### [171] [Actions Speak Louder than Words: Measuring Cross-Lingual Policy Retention in Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11110)
*Sourabrata Mukherjee,Kalika Bali,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将工具使用智能体的"动作策略"（而非最终答案）作为跨语言评估的测量对象，在8个模型、6个平行基准、41种语言共238万次 rollout 上进行研究。研究发现直觉测量存在5个混杂因素，逐一修正后跨语言一致性结论反而更强：大模型在贪心解码下保留71-73%的动作策略，模型身份仅解释5.7%的方差；小型模型（<10B参数）的一致性崩溃，且小模型排序多由偶然性下限决定；非英语任务被路由到英语处理，且该行为具有因果负载作用。此外，单一的正则提取bug可将一个模型测得准确率放大26倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言评估通常只比较最终答案而丢弃动作序列，但动作才是系统真正产出（决定成本、延迟、失败方式与可审计性）。作者希望将动作策略本身作为被测量的对象，检验工具使用智能体在不同语言下是否执行相同步骤。

Method: 在8个模型、6个平行基准、41种语言上执行238万次 rollout。采用轨迹相似度作为测量指标，系统性地识别并逐一消除5个混杂因素：短轨迹得分虚高、空轨迹完美得分、无关轨迹偶合一致（>50%概率）、模型自身可复现性上限、同语言重复提问本身不一致导致无基线。利用置换检验测量偶然性下限（而非假设），并通过预注册预测验证英语枢轴（pivot）假说。

Result: 每个混杂因素的修正都使跨语言效应变大。分歧是结构性的而非采样噪声：贪心解码下所有单元都仍存在分歧，随温度升高保持平坦（即使模型自一致性下降）。按自身可复现性归一化后，4个不同的前沿模型在贪心解码下收敛，各自保留71-73%的动作策略跨语言一致性，模型身份仅解释5.7%的方差。低于约10B参数时一致性崩溃，小模型间的排序主要是偶然性下限的伪影。代理将非英语任务路由至英语，此枢轴因果负载，且模型被告知也不会放弃。一个轨迹提取正则表达式bug可令某模型测得准确率提升26倍。

Conclusion: 跨语言动作策略的不一致性在主流模型中比先前认知的更小且具有结构性，主要受限于模型自身可复现性而非语言差异；但该一致性在小模型上崩溃且可能被测量噪声掩盖。英语枢轴是工具使用代理跨语言行为的关键机制。测量中的工程细节（如轨迹提取）可能比模型本身更能决定结果，提示多语言评估必须将动作（而非仅答案）作为一等公民，并严格控制测量混杂。

Abstract: When a tool-using agent is given the same task in a different language, does it still take the same steps? Multilingual evaluation rarely asks: it compares final answers and discards the actions. Yet those actions are the product: they fix cost and latency, decide how the system fails, and are the only auditable part of its behaviour. We make the action policy the measured object across 8 models, 6 parallel benchmarks and 41 languages (2.38M rollouts). The naive measurement fails: five confounds sit between raw trace similarity and any defensible claim, each able to flip a conclusion. Short traces score higher, empty traces score perfectly, unrelated traces agree by chance over half the time, the gap is capped by each model's reproducibility, and a model asked the same question twice in one language answers differently, leaving no baseline. We remove all five, and every correction makes the effect larger. Divergence proves structural, not sampling noise: it survives greedy decoding in every cell and stays flat as temperature rises, even as models grow less self-consistent. Normalised by their own reproducibility, four very different frontier models converge under greedy decoding, each keeping 71-73% of its action policy across languages, with model identity explaining only 5.7% of the variance. Below roughly 10B parameters it breaks down, and the ordering among smaller models is largely an artifact of a chance floor we measure by permutation rather than assume. Agents route non-English tasks through English; this pivot is causally load-bearing, confirmed by a pre-registered prediction across four models, and models will not abandon it when told to. Finally, a single trace-extraction regex, not the model, manufactured a multilingual failure: two worked examples raise one model's measured accuracy twenty-sixfold while its accuracy on readable outputs barely moves.

</details>


### [172] [Attention-Path Fragility as an Uncertainty Signal in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11138)
*Minsoo Kim,Sungyoung Ji,Kisung Moon,Ilyong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ASMI（注意力子网络互信息），一种无需训练的估计器，通过掩码注意力头并测量子网络间的BALD互信息（结合语义一致性核）来捕捉"自信但脆弱"的预测信号，该信号在基于上下文的问答中能补充单次置信度和熵之外的误差预测信息，并在语义熵基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性估计主要依赖输出分布的宽度（如置信度、熵），但模型对某个token的不确定性不仅体现在分布广度上，还体现在自信预测在注意力路径扰动下是否脆弱。作者希望捕捉这种"自信但脆弱"的预测信号，以提升误差预测能力，特别是在基于上下文的问答任务中。

Method: ASMI通过掩码注意力头生成多个子网络，计算这些子网络输出之间的BALD互信息，并使用语义一致性核来过滤表面形式不一致带来的干扰。Sem-ASMI变体可从单次贪心解码中读取信号，无需随机采样生成；自适应变体可复用基线方法已采样的样本。方法无需训练，即插即用。

Result: 在基于上下文的问答中，ASMI在置信度过滤器之外补充了误差预测信息，可将保留误差减少约一半。在12个grounded基准-骨干网络设置中，Sem-ASMI在10个设置上达到或超过语义熵；最优ASMI变体在8个设置上达到或领先最强基线，其中3个在配对检验下显著。在参数化QA上所有变体退化为零成本MSP基线，与预测一致。头部级分析表明：跟踪边界的关键不是头部级脆弱性的存在，而是该脆弱性是否与错误耦合。

Conclusion: ASMI提供了一种新颖的不确定性信号维度——"自信但脆弱"，它不同于传统置信度/熵信号。其适用域是可预测的：在答案通过上下文路由时有效，在从参数知识回忆时受限。实验验证了"脆弱性-错误耦合"而非单纯脆弱性是误差预测的关键机制。

Abstract: We propose that a model's uncertainty about a token is reflected not only in the breadth of its output distribution but also in whether a confident prediction is \emph{fragile} under perturbation of its attention pathways. We instantiate this as ASMI (Attention-Subnetwork Mutual Information), a training-free estimator that masks attention heads and measures the BALD mutual information among the resulting subnetworks, with a semantic-agreement kernel to discount surface-form disagreement. The signal is not a restatement of output confidence: on grounded QA an out-of-fold test shows it adds error-predictive information beyond single-pass confidence and entropy, concentrated in \emph{confident-but-fragile} predictions, where acting on it roughly halves the retained error of a confidence filter. The distinctness is regime-graded, so ASMI predicts its own domain of applicability, strong where answers are routed through provided context and bounded by design where they are recalled from parametric knowledge. Sem-ASMI reads the signal from a single greedy response, without the stochastic generations the strongest baselines require, and ties or beats Semantic Entropy on ten of the twelve grounded benchmark-backbone settings. Across the same twelve settings, the best ASMI variant, typically the adaptive one reusing the ten samples already drawn for the baselines, ties or leads the strongest baseline in eight, significantly in three under a paired test. On parametric QA all variants revert to or below the zero-cost MSP baseline, exactly as predicted, and the estimates are near-deterministic across reruns. A head-level analysis shows that what tracks this boundary is not the presence of head-level fragility but whether that fragility couples to errors.

</details>


### [173] [The Illusion of Cross-Lingual Safety in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11146)
*Abigail Oppong,P Sam Sahil,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Maryam Ibrahim Mukhtar,Esmael Ahmed Abdu,Tassallah Abdullahi,Jessica Oparebea,Saminu Mohammad Aliyu,Idris Abdulmumin,Abubakar Juma Chilala,Nicholaus Dismas Ladislaus,Alfred Malengo Kondoro,Lemofouet Valdini Douglace,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Seid Muhie Yimam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型在多语言场景下的安全对齐问题，聚焦四种非洲低资源语言（Twi、豪萨语、阿姆哈拉语和斯瓦希里语）。通过构建LoDNA安全数据集，并提出了一个基于隐状态拒绝表示的空间几何框架来评估跨语言安全迁移，发现跨语言安全迁移严重受限，有害提示在大多数模型-语言对中保留的英文拒绝信号不足10%，证明了当前多语言安全对齐是表面化的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的安全对齐主要在英语环境中开发和评估，默认这些安全机制可以跨语言泛化。然而这一假设在多语言环境下很少得到验证，特别是在低资源语言中存在明显漏洞。作者希望系统性地评估这种跨语言安全迁移的真实能力。

Method: 1) 构建LoDNA安全数据集，包含字面翻译和文化本土化提示的成对数据；2) 提出一个潜在几何框架，探测LLM隐藏状态中的"拒绝表示"（refusal representations），超越传统的生成式评估方法；3) 在四种非洲语言上执行实验，分析来自英文拒绝信号在跨语言环境中的保留程度、语义对齐度和层间漂移。

Result: 跨语言安全迁移严重受限：有害提示在大多数模型-语言对中保留的英文拒绝信号不足10%。字面翻译和本土化提示在语义上高度对齐（余弦相似度0.95-0.996），但在各层之间发生漂移，说明模型编码了相关概念却没有将其路由到安全机制中。

Conclusion: 当前多语言安全对齐是表面化的，这些发现有力反驳了在这些低资源语言中存在通用的、与语言无关的有害流形（harm manifold）的假设。研究需警惕论文中包含可能冒犯性或有害的示例数据。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is largely developed in English, assuming these safeguards generalize across multilingual settings. However, this assumption remains underexplored and exposes a vulnerability in low-resource languages. We investigate cross-lingual safety transfer in four African languages, Twi, Hausa, Amharic, and Swahili, using LoDNA, a new safety dataset that pairs literal translations with culturally localized prompts. To move beyond generation-based evaluation, we propose a latent geometric framework that probes hidden-state refusal representations in LLMs. Our experimental results show that cross-lingual safety transfer is severely limited; harmful prompts retain less than 10% of the English refusal signal across most language-model pairs. Literal and localized prompts are semantically aligned (cosine 0.95-0.996) but drift across layers, suggesting models encode the concepts without routing them to safety mechanisms. These findings demonstrate that current multilingual safety alignment is superficial, providing strong evidence against the assumption of a universal, language-agnostic harm manifold within the specific low-resource languages studied. Warning: This paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [174] [From Interpretability to Control: Insights from Six Years of the TrustNLP Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11171)
*Rahul Gupta,Abhinav Mohanty,Anaelia Ovalle,Anil Ramakrishna,Anubrata Das,Apurv Verma,Jwala Dhamala,Ninareh Mehrabi,Tharindu Kumarage,Yada Pruksachatkun,Yang Trista Cao,Kai-Wei Chang,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对TrustNLP研讨会六届共144篇论文进行系统综述，沿六个信任维度分类，发现可信NLP领域已从静态模型的事后可解释性转向生成式系统的机制理解与主动控制，并识别出真实性增长最快、公平性最稳定、可解释性呈U形轨迹等关键趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式大语言模型的快速演进，可信NLP领域经历了显著的方向转变。作者旨在通过全面梳理TrustNLP研讨会六届论文，系统刻画该领域从可解释性到可信生成的发展脉络，并为社区提供结构化的研究洞察与未来方向。

Method: 作者对TrustNLP研讨会（2021年起与ACL主要会议同期举办）全部144篇论文进行分类分析，基于TrustLLM和DecodingTrust等成熟框架建立六个信任维度，并交叉对比同期ACL、NAACL、EACL、EMNLP约2000篇论文的专题分布。

Result: 发现：(1) 首个高影响力聊天模型的发布同时激活了所有信任维度，后续模型代际将焦点转向真实性与安全对齐；(2) 真实性是增长最快的维度（2021-2022年缺席，2025-2026年占37%）；(3) 公平性是最稳定的主题；(4) 可解释性呈U形轨迹——事后方法衰退后在2026年借机械可解释性复苏；(5) TrustNLP的专题分布与领域平均水平高度一致。论文数量从8篇增长至41篇。

Conclusion: 该领域已从静态模型的事后可解释性过渡到生成式系统的机制理解与主动控制。作者提出四项结构性洞察，并为研究社区给出可操作的方向建议。

Abstract: The Workshop on Trustworthy Natural Language Processing (TrustNLP), co-located with major ACL conferences since 2021, has grown from 8 proceedings papers to 41 over six editions, documenting a field-wide transition from post-hoc interpretability of static models to mechanistic understanding and proactive control of generative systems. We synthesize insights from all 144 proceedings papers, classifying them along six trust dimensions grounded in established frameworks (TrustLLM, DecodingTrust). We observe co-occurrences with capability emergence. The release of the first high-impact chat models activated all trust dimensions simultaneously, while subsequent model generations shifted focus toward truthfulness and safety alignment. Analysis from the classification study reveals that truthfulness is the fastest-growing dimension (absent in 2021-2022, comprising 37% of papers by 2025-2026), fairness remains the most consistent theme, and explainability exhibits a U-shaped trajectory; declining as post-hoc methods lost relevance but resurging in 2026 through mechanistic interpretability. A cross-venue comparison with ACL, NAACL, EACL, and EMNLP (~2K papers) in the same period shows that TrustNLP's topical distribution closely follows the field average. We identify four structural insights and conclude with actionable directions for the research community.

</details>


### [175] [ConVAWG: A Retrieval-Grounded Framework for Controlled Synthetic Dialogue Generation in Violence Against Women and Girls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11200)
*Chen Lyu,Xingwei Tan,Simon Cullen,Shelley Wilson,Lois Arthurs,Arshad Jhumka,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConVAWG，一个基于检索增强的合成对话生成框架，用于建模针对妇女和女童的暴力（VAWG）多轮对话场景，生成了6000多个对话事件，并经过人工评估和下游任务验证展示了良好的对话质量与领域忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实对话数据在敏感领域难以获取、发布和标注，现有工作大多聚焦于句子级在线毒性检测，缺乏将虐待建模为关系性和随时间展开的会话现象的尝试。

Method: ConVAWG框架：从人物种子、英国国家统计局的人口统计模式、官方犯罪定义和检索到的家庭凶杀审查案例构建场景；转换为分层事件时间线；生成多场景角色扮演对话；并应用基于激活引导的毒性控制。

Result: 发布了涵盖200个场景、超过6000个多轮对话事件的数据集，附有丰富的场景级、事件级和轮次级元数据。人工评估、LLM-as-Judge评估、消融实验和下游任务均展现了较强的对话质量和领域忠实度。

Conclusion: 合成对话生成为敏感领域中研究对话动态提供了一种可行途径，ConVAWG在建模VAWG多轮对话方面填补了句子级毒性检测与关系性、时间性虐待建模之间的空白。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogue generation offers a way to study conversational dynamics in sensitive domains where real data are difficult to access, release, or annotate. The underlying abuse may occur online or offline: threats and coercion can appear directly in messages, while behaviours such as surveillance, isolation, stalking, and physical violence may be planned, disclosed, or referred to conversationally. Privacy and legal constraints make it difficult the release of large-scale real conversation datasets; existing work has mostly focused on sentence-level toxicity of online abuses, leaving a gap in modelling abuse as a relational and temporally unfolding phenomenon. In this work, we focus on modelling Violence Against Women and Girls (VAWG) scenarios as multi-turn dialogues. We introduce ConVAWG, a retrieval-grounded framework for generating CPS-aligned synthetic VAWG chat dialogues. ConVAWG builds scenarios from persona seeds, demographic patterns reported by the UK Office for National Statistics, official crime definitions, and retrieved Domestic Homicide Review cases; converts them into hierarchical event timelines; generates multi-scene role-play dialogues; and applies targeted activation-steered toxicity control to appropriate utterances. We release over 6,000 multi-turn dialogue events across 200 scenarios with rich scenario-, event-, and turn-level metadata. Extensive human evaluation, LLM-as-Judge assessment, ablations, and downstream tasks show strong dialogue quality and domain fidelity.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Hand-Written PTX Tensor-Core GEMM Kernels: A Multi-Precision Study on NVIDIA L4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10103)
*Matt J. Borowski,Blazej Osinski*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在NVIDIA L4 GPU（Ada/SM89）上系统对比了WMMA C++ API与手写PTX实现的GEMM内核在FP16、INT8、INT4三种精度下的性能。结果表明：FP16下PTX无端到端加速（指令级收益被操作数打包开销抵消）；INT8下PTX实现1.4x-1.8x加速（源于更低指令数和更好的全局内存合并）；INT4下PTX实现2.9x-4.3x加速（原生mma.sync.m16n8k64.s4规避了WMMA的软件模拟序列）。大矩阵下吞吐量更受内存子系统行为而非Tensor Core利用率或占用率影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Tensor Core内核性能依赖底层PTX管线（cp.async、ldmatrix、mma.sync），但大多数应用代码通过WMMA C++ API间接访问Tensor Core。作者希望回答一个实用问题：何时用手写PTX替代WMMA能真正带来性能回报，从而为开发者提供明确的精度和规模选择依据。

Method: 在单块NVIDIA L4 GPU上进行受控实验，对比双缓冲WMMA基线内核与一组手写PTX GEMM内核，覆盖FP16、INT8、INT4三种精度，方阵规模从N=512到N=8192。所有内核均用Nsight Compute采集完整指标集，PTX加速比相对于同精度WMMA基线计算。

Result: FP16下PTX无端到端加速；INT8下PTX实现1.4x-1.8x一致加速（归因于更低的指令数和更好的全局内存合并）；INT4下PTX实现2.9x-4.3x加速（原生s4执行避免WMMA的软件模拟）。相对FP16 WMMA基线，最佳量化内核在N=8192时达34.4x（INT8）和98.7x（INT4）。此外，占用率不是吞吐量的良好预测因子，大矩阵下性能更紧密地跟随全局加载合并与DRAM活跃周期等内存系统行为。

Conclusion: 手写PTX的回报具有精度和操作规模依赖性：FP16场景下不值得付出额外复杂性；INT8和INT4量化场景下值得，且规模越大收益越显著。研究结果明确了手写PTX复杂性的适用精度与运行区间，为Tensor Core内核开发提供了实用指导。

Abstract: High-performance Tensor Core kernels rely on a low-level PTX pipeline built from asynchronous data movement with cp.async, warp-level matrix loads with ldmatrix, and matrix multiply-accumulate operations with mma.sync. However, most application code accesses Tensor Cores indirectly through the WMMA C++ API. This paper asks a focused, practical question: when does replacing WMMA with hand-written PTX actually pay off? To answer this question, we conduct a controlled, single-GPU study on an NVIDIA L4 GPU (Ada, SM89), comparing double-buffered WMMA baselines with a family of hand-written PTX GEMM kernels across FP16, INT8, and INT4 arithmetic and square problem sizes from $N=512$ to $N=8192$. Every kernel is profiled with Nsight Compute across the full metric set, and PTX speedups are reported relative to the corresponding same-precision WMMA baseline. Hand-written PTX provides no end-to-end speedup for FP16, because its instruction-level gains are offset by operand-packing overhead. In contrast, the PTX kernels achieve consistent speedups of 1.4x-1.8x for INT8, driven primarily by lower instruction counts and better global-memory coalescing, and 2.9x-4.3x for INT4, where native mma.sync.m16n8k64.s4 execution avoids the software-emulated sequence used by the WMMA path. Relative to the FP16 WMMA baseline, the best quantized kernels reach 34.4x (INT8) and 98.7x (INT4) at $N=8192$. Across these experiments, occupancy is a poor predictor of throughput. For large matrices, performance instead tracks memory-system behavior -- particularly global-load coalescing and DRAM-active cycles -- more closely than Tensor Core utilization. These results identify the precisions and operating regimes in which the additional complexity of hand-written PTX is justified.

</details>


### [177] [What Actually Serializes GPU LZ77 Decode: Three Decoders, Three Mechanisms, and an Encode-Time Lever That Removes the Last One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10188)
*Yakiv Shavidze*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性测量推翻了一个普遍假设：GPU LZ77解码的瓶颈并非copy阶段而是parse阶段（占设备端解码时间64-72%）。作者提出了可证明的、影响极小的多项优化（限制回引用链深度、并行化自重叠匹配、去除距离历史依赖），并揭示了格式本身的带宽效率瓶颈（总线效率仅4.4%）。所有可复现声明均附带机器可验证的检查记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU LZ77解码的并行化研究中，学界普遍假设copy阶段是主要瓶颈。作者通过实际测量发现该假设错误——parse阶段才是真正的时间瓶颈（占64-72%），并系统性反驳了与这一假设相关的十个研究假设，包括纠正自身的方法论错误。

Method: 在H100上对三种解码器架构进行剖析测量；通过证明限制回引用链深度代价仅0.006%压缩比且对涉及文件延迟尖峰的181个块无影响（逐字节对比全部15,499个块）；将自重叠匹配建模为周期性填充而非依赖链实现全并行（位精确）；通过编码端移除四项距离历史（代价0.540%压缩比），将无依赖parse命令数从4提升到706；并采用带机器检查记录的可复现实验方法（17/17检查通过）。

Result: parse占解码时间64-72%；限制链深度对延迟影响不超过2.8%且关键尖峰完全不变；自重叠匹配并行化加速match层2.75-8.42x（位精确）；移除距离历史后无依赖指令从4增至706；格式底层瓶颈为：中位匹配7字节对128字节缓存行，总线效率仅4.4%，合并写入快39x；十个研究假设被反驳。

Conclusion: GPU LZ77解码的瓶颈在parse而非copy，且格式本身的短匹配与缓存行不匹配构成更深层的硬件效率天花板。通过编码端与解码端的协同设计，可以将解码路径中几乎所有的顺序依赖消除，但格式固有的带宽低效仍是实际硬性限制。

Abstract: The sequential part of GPU LZ77 decode is not where the field assumes it is. Across three decoder architectures on an H100 we measure that parse, not copy, holds 64-72% of device-resident decode time; that bounding back-reference chain depth - provable, and costing 0.006% in ratio - moves latency by at most 2.8% and, for the file's own latency spike, provably by nothing at all, since a byte-level comparison of all 15,499 blocks shows the cap alters none of the 181 blocks involved; that self-overlapping matches are periodic fills rather than dependency chains, which makes them fully parallel and speeds the match layer by 2.75-8.42x bit-perfect; and that the last genuinely sequential element, a four-entry distance history, can be removed by the encoder for 0.540% of ratio, growing the dependency-free parse run from 4 commands to 706. We also report the floor the format runs into: with a median match of 7 bytes against a 128-byte cache line, bus efficiency is 4.4% and a coalesced write of the same data is 39x faster. A separate section records ten hypotheses these measurements refuted, including one methodological error of our own. Every reproducible claim carries a machine-checkable record: a fresh clone of the tagged release passes 17 of 17 checks reachable without a GPU, none failing.

</details>


### [178] [FaCTz: Fast Critical-Point and Topology-Aware GPU Compression for Scientific Vector Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10586)
*Mingze Xia,Yuxiao Li,Sheng Di,Jiannan Tian,Baixi Sun,Boyi Zhang,Bei Wang,Hanqi Guo,Xin Liang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FaCTz是首个基于GPU的、保证临界点（critical point）保存的有损压缩器。它将固有顺序约束的临界点保持问题重构为独立并行任务（按块或按点推测），在三个向量场数据集上实现了高达60 GB/s的吞吐量（比CPU版cpSZ快约640倍），同时其推测模式相比吞吐量导向模式压缩比提升约两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模科学模拟产生的向量场数据需要误差有界有损压缩来存储与传输，但现有有损压缩器虽限制数值误差，却不保证场的拓扑结构（临界点可能因微小扰动被创建或消除），而下游特征分析依赖这些临界点。现有GPU压缩器吞吐量高但忽略拓扑，唯一具有可证明临界点保持能力的压缩器cpSZ运行在CPU上，吞吐量远低于现代GPU系统的数据生成速率。

Method: FaCTz观察到虽然临界点保持本质上是耦合且顺序的约束，但可以将其重构为独立并行任务：一种基于块的模式（面向吞吐量优化）和一种推测性的基于点的模式（面向压缩比优化）。两种模式均在GPU上实现，从而同时利用并行能力保证临界点不丢失。

Result: 在三个向量场数据集上，FaCTz在保持所有临界点的同时达到最高60 GB/s的吞吐量，比多线程CPU实现cpSZ快约两个数量级（最高约640倍）。推测模式相比吞吐量模式进一步将压缩比提升约两倍。

Conclusion: FaCTz证明临界点保持约束可以被高效地并行化并部署在GPU上，首次实现了兼具高吞吐量（匹配现代GPU系统）与可证明拓扑保持的有损压缩，为大规模科学模拟数据的实时压缩与传输提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Error-bounded lossy compression is essential for storing and transferring the vector-field data produced by large-scale scientific simulations. Although it enforces a user-specified error bound to limit numerical distortion, it does not preserve the field's topology: small admissible perturbations can create or eliminate critical points on which downstream feature analysis depends. Existing GPU compressors achieve high throughput but are topology-agnostic, whereas the only compressor with provable critical-point preservation (cpSZ) runs on the CPU at throughput far below the data-generation rates of modern GPU-based systems. We observe that, although preserving critical points is inherently a coupled and sequential constraint, it can be reformulated into independent parallel tasks, either on a per-block basis or, speculatively, on a per-point basis. We present FaCTz, the first GPU-based error-bounded lossy compressor that guarantees critical-point preservation. FaCTz provides a block-wise mode optimized for throughput and a speculative per-point mode optimized for compression ratio. Across three vector-field datasets, FaCTz preserves every critical point while achieving throughput of up to 60 GB/s, approximately two orders of magnitude (up to approximately 640x) faster than the multithreaded CPU implementation of cpSZ. Its speculative mode further improves the compression ratio by approximately a factor of two over the throughput-oriented mode.

</details>


### [179] [ClusterBench: A Framework for Cluster-Wide Continuous Benchmarking and Regression Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10956)
*Aditya Ujeniya,Jan Eitzinger,Thomas Gruber,Georg Hager,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ClusterBench，一个集群级持续基准测试框架，用于在验收及后续定期对数据中心整套安装进行验证。它向集群中每个节点分派相同基准测试（覆盖CPU、GPU、内存、互连和I/O），并在集群生命周期内反复执行，从而收集跨空间和时间的数据，用于检测软件变更导致的性能回归，并形成硬件变异性研究数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心需要能够验证整个安装而非单个节点的工具，应用于验收阶段及后续定期检查。这要求将相同的基准测试分派到每个节点，因此需要集群感知的调度机制，而现有工具缺乏这种能力。

Method: ClusterBench框架采用集群感知调度，将相同的基准测试集（涵盖CPU、GPU、内存、互连和I/O）在一次提交中分发到所有节点。由于测量在集群生命周期内持续重复，系统可跨空间和时间收集数据；通过与历史运行结果对比检测性能回归，并将数据与功耗、频率和温度等指标进行相关性分析。

Result: 在NHR@FAU集群（Helma、Alex和Fritz）上的实验显示：同一组件内的性能变化保持在1%以内；尽管节点规格完全相同，不同个体之间的差异可达5%。性能与功耗、频率、温度的相关性在风冷和液冷节点之间存在差异。

Conclusion: ClusterBench证明了集群级持续基准测试的有效性，能够检测软件更新（如内核升级或新库版本）引入的性能回归，同时生成的跨空间时间数据集可作为硬件变异性研究的基础。冷却方式（风冷vs液冷）会影响性能与功耗、频率、温度之间的关系。

Abstract: Data centers need tooling that validates an entire installation rather than individual nodes, at acceptance and at regular intervals thereafter. This requires dispatching identical benchmarks to every node in a single submission, and therefore cluster-aware scheduling. This paper presents ClusterBench, a framework for cluster-wide continuous benchmarking. It ships with a benchmark collection targeting each component: CPU, GPU, memory, interconnect, and I/O. Because measurements are repeated throughout the cluster's lifetime, ClusterBench collects data across space and time. Comparison against earlier runs detects performance regressions introduced by software changes, such as kernel updates or new library versions. The measurements also form a dataset for research on hardware variability. On the NHR@FAU clusters Helma, Alex, and Fritz, variation within a single component stays within 1%. Variation across specimens reaches 5%, despite nodes identical by specification. Correlating performance with power draw, frequency, and temperature shows that this relationship differs between air- and liquid-cooled nodes.

</details>


### [180] [Workflow Cards: Structured Summaries of Workflow Executions Using Provenance Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11022)
*Nicola Giuseppe Marchioro,Gabriele Padovani,Amal Gueroudji,Rafael Ferreira da Silva,Wesley Brewer,Valentine Anantharaj,Sandro Fiore,Renan Souza*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Workflow Cards（工作流卡片），一种结构化的、人类和LLM可读的工作流执行摘要文档，用于弥补Model Cards和Data Cards忽略工作流执行细节的不足。实验表明，Workflow Cards提供的执行级信息填补了现有文档空白，且相比基于schema的数据库查询，将LLM回答质量提升近两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Model Cards和Data Cards虽然为机器学习工件提供了结构化文档，但只关注静态工件（数据集和训练模型），忽略了产生、转换和评估这些工件的工作流执行过程。而数据准备、参数选择、运行时行为、资源使用和中间转换等关键细节恰恰是偏差、性能波动和可复现性问题的根源所在。

Method: 论文包含两部分：首先，基于从Model Cards和Data Cards中缺失的执行级数据中提炼出的具有代表性的溯源问题集，定义了Workflow Card模板；其次，评估LLM使用Workflow Cards理解工作流执行的效率，与通过schema接口查询溯源数据库的方式进行了对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，Workflow Cards能够提供Model Cards和Data Cards所缺失的执行级信息，填补了重要的文档空白；与schema查询相比，Workflow Cards在LLM-as-a-Judge和人类评估两种方式下均一致地将回答质量提升近两倍。

Conclusion: Workflow Cards作为一种新的文档形式，有效弥补了现有机器学习工件文档体系的不足，增强了人类和LLM对工作流执行过程的理解与分析能力，对提升可复现性和可解释性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Model Cards and Data Cards have demonstrated the value of structured, human-readable documentation for machine learning artifacts, capturing their context, parameters, limitations, and intended use. However, these practices remain focused on static artifacts (the datasets and trained models themselves) while overlooking the workflow executions that produce, transform, and evaluate them. Such executions hold critical details about data preparation, parameter choice, runtime behavior, resource use, and intermediate transformations, precisely where bias, performance variation, and reproducibility gaps tend to originate. To close this gap, we introduce Workflow Cards: structured summaries that condense the machine-readable provenance data of a workflow execution into a form both humans and large language models (LLMs) can read and analyze. This paper has two main parts. First, it defines a Workflow Card template informed by a representative set of provenance questions that surface from the execution-level data missing from Model and Data Cards. Second, it evaluates how effectively LLMs use Workflow Cards to understand workflow executions compared with querying provenance databases through a schema-based interface. Results show that Workflow Cards provide execution-level information absent from existing card types, such as Model Cards and Data Cards, thereby filling an important documentation gap; and that Workflow Cards nearly double answer quality compared with schema-based querying, consistently across LLM-as-a-Judge and human assessments.

</details>


### [181] [SCOUT: Symmetric Consensus Outlier Detection for Failure Localization in LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11034)
*Zhuang Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个统一的大语言模型预训练故障定位框架，基于"严格多数共识"原则，通过对等副本的进度、时序和数值对齐来识别离群rank，并通过C3通信抽象、带外CPU观察器和原位重放等技术，在训练卡死或崩溃后仍能定位故障根源（如straggler、SDC），且无需修改训练循环或框架源码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练中，同步机制会将rank局部的停滞、减速和数值错误传播为全局症状，使故障根源难以定位。现有方案（进程内监控、事后日志、离线健康测试）都有明显缺陷：进程内监控在训练器阻塞或终止后无法报告；事后日志只能记录同步后的症状；离线测试丢失了触发故障的工作负载和运行环境。

Method: SCOUT构建在"通过对等副本严格多数共识识别离群者"这一核心设计原则上。它对齐副本的进度、时序和数值证据，使用C3（Consensus Collective Communication）抽象识别紧凑签名与同伴不一致的rank。采用带外CPU观察器在训练挂起时保持响应，同时利用原位重放（在实时作业旁边，以相同的模型状态、kernel、分配、通信路径及热/内存压力）来复现周期性straggler和SDC。集体指纹暴露rank本地的协议分歧，干净重放覆盖为检查点数值完整性提供认证。

Result: SCOUT成功实现统一的运行时故障定位，可识别进度、时序和数值层面的离群rank；在训练挂起时带外观察器仍能保持响应并报告；原位重放能够复现周期性的straggler和SDC；干净重放覆盖可验证检查点数值完整性，防止从被SDC破坏的状态恢复。SCOUT已与PyTorch、TorchTitan、Megatron-Core和DeepSpeed集成，无需修改训练循环或框架源码，并已开源。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过严格多数共识和C3抽象提供了一个统一、可部署的LLM预训练故障定位方案，克服了现有监控在阻塞/终止场景下的失效问题，并能在真实工作负载和运行条件下识别故障根源。作为开源项目，它为大规模LLM训练的可观测性和弹性提供了一个实用基础。

Abstract: In LLM pre-training, synchronization propagates rank-local stalls, slowdowns, and numerical errors into job-wide symptoms, obscuring their origin. Existing diagnosis often relies on in-process monitors that cannot report after the trainer blocks or terminates, or on post-mortem logs that preserve only synchronized symptoms; offline health tests lose the workload and operating conditions that triggered the failure. We present SCOUT, a unified runtime failure-localization framework built on one design principle: identify outliers through strict-majority consensus among equivalent replicas. SCOUT aligns replica progress, timing, and numerical evidence, then uses its Consensus Collective Communication (C3) abstraction to identify ranks whose compact signatures disagree with their peers. An out-of-band CPU observer remains responsive when training hangs, whereas in-situ replay exercises recurring stragglers and silent data corruption (SDC) beside the live job with its model state, kernels, allocations, communication path, and thermal and memory pressure present. Collective fingerprints expose rank-local protocol divergence. Clean replay coverage certifies checkpoint numerical integrity, preventing recovery from selecting state corrupted by SDC. SCOUT integrates with PyTorch, TorchTitan, Megatron-Core, and DeepSpeed without training-loop or framework-source modifications. SCOUT is open source at https://github.com/LMResiliency/lm-resiliency.

</details>


### [182] [Scheduling Mixed RL Rollouts Beyond Prefix Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11152)
*Zetao Hong,Song Yuan,Yuanhao Ding,Yibo Zhu,Daxin Jiang,Zhibin Wang,Chen Tian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MISA-T 是一种针对混合域大规模语言模型强化学习后训练的服务路由层准入策略，通过自适应会话准入、工作负载感知的 KV 容量分配和驻留时间感知的 KV 计费，在异构 rollout 会话共存时提升推理吞吐量并保持工作负载混合比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 的 RL 后训练流水线同时处理来自多个域（RLVR、RLHF、agentic rollouts）的异构 rollout 工作负载，这些会话在序列结构、交互模式和 KV 驻留时间上差异巨大。传统的前缀感知路由虽能提升缓存复用和负载均衡，但未控制异构会话对 KV 缓存容量的竞争，导致推理服务效率下降。因此需要一种能感知异构性、同时不扭曲训练器指定工作负载混合的调度策略。

Method: MISA-T 由三部分组成：1) 自适应会话准入（adaptive session admission），控制何时接纳新的 rollout 会话；2) 工作负载感知的 KV 容量分配（workload-aware KV-capacity allocation），根据不同域的工作负载特性分配 KV 缓存资源；3) 驻留时间感知的 KV 计费（residency-time-aware KV accounting），将 KV 驻留时长纳入资源核算。

Result: 在 Step3.7 和 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 上的 rollout 消融实验中，相比经过扫描调优的 vLLM 缓存感知路由器，MISA-T 分别提升 rollout 吞吐量 53.3% 和 43.6%，同时保持较高的前缀缓存命中率。在匹配的 50 迭代 Step3.7 实验中，rollout 吞吐量提升 35.6%，平均迭代时间减少 22.8%，同时消耗的工作负载混合比例接近训练器目标，任务得分相当。

Conclusion: MISA-T 有效解决了异构 rollout 会话共享推理服务时的 KV 缓存竞争问题，在不扭曲训练器指定的工作负载混合的前提下显著提升了吞吐量和训练效率，证明了路由层的准入与容量管理策略对现代 RL 后训练管线的重要性。

Abstract: Modern reinforcement learning (RL) post-training pipelines for large language models (LLMs) increasingly combine rollout workloads across multiple domains and feedback paradigms. Prefix-aware routing improves inference efficiency through cache reuse and load balancing, but it does not control how heterogeneous rollout sessions compete for KV-cache capacity. When reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), and agentic rollouts share an asynchronous inference service, their distinct sequence structures, interaction patterns, and KV-residency times create substantially different serving demands. Rollout scheduling must account for this heterogeneity without distorting the workload mixture specified by the trainer. We present MISA-T, a routing-layer admission policy for mixed rollout serving. MISA-T combines adaptive session admission, workload-aware KV-capacity allocation, and residency-time-aware KV accounting. In rollout-only ablations on Step3.7 and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, MISA-T improves rollout throughput over a sweep-tuned cache-aware vLLM Router by 53.3% and 43.6%, respectively, while maintaining high prefix-cache hit rates. In a matched 50-iteration Step3.7 experiment, it increases rollout throughput by 35.6% and reduces mean iteration time by 22.8%, while keeping the consumed workload mixture close to the trainer target and achieving comparable task scores.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-IV: Developmental Topology Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09997)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TGO-IV，一个基于持久同调的拓扑框架，用于系统分析Transformer各层间表征流形（representation point cloud）的演变过程，通过多种拓扑不变量揭示原始输入表征如何逐步演化成任务相关特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer可解释性研究主要分析孤立层或整个网络的表征，但缺乏对单个表征及其流形在跨层动态演化过程的深入理解。作者旨在填补这一空白，从拓扑视角理解Transformer是如何学习的。

Method: 从token级表征点云构建Vietoris-Rips单纯复形，并利用持久同调提取拓扑签名，包含六个互补的拓扑观测器：持久性图、条形图、Betti曲线、持久性景观、Bottleneck距离和Wasserstein距离，综合分析表征点云全局拓扑在前向传播中的演变。

Result: 通过多种拓扑不变量揭示了Transformer各层之间表征点云的全局拓扑发展规律，能够识别出原始输入表征何时及如何转变为任务相关特征表征的特定层或趋势。

Conclusion: TGO-IV证明持久同调是分析Transformer表征演化的有效拓扑框架，补充了传统局部几何分析方法的不足，为从全局拓扑角度理解Transformer学习机制提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Transformers have had a profound impact on the world of language processing and computer vision. As efforts to answer the million-dollar question of ``How does a Transformer learn?" have been increasing, existing interpretability studies primarily analyze representations at isolated layers or the network as a whole, while the developmental evolution of individual representations and its manifolds across transformer layers remains underexplored. With this work, we aim at providing a comprehensive analysis of the evolution of representations as the representation point cloud transforms across the layers; thereby attempting to isolate layers or establish a trend which comes closer to justifying how and when raw input representations evolve into task-relevant feature representations. Thus, Transformer Geometry Observatory-TGO-IV introduces a topological framework for analysing the evolution of Transformer representations through the lens of Persistent Homology. Rather than studying local geometric properties alone, TGO-IV constructs Vietoris--Rips simplicial complexes from token-level representation point clouds and investigates the evolution of their persistent topological signatures across Transformer layers. The proposed framework comprises complementary topological observatories including Persistence Diagrams, Barcode Diagrams, Betti Curves, Persistence Landscapes, Bottleneck Distance, and Wasserstein Distance, enabling a comprehensive analysis of how the global topology of representation point clouds develops throughout the forward pass.

</details>


### [184] [Uncertainty-Aware Ensemble Deep Randomized Neural Networks for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10007)
*M. Sajid,A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,P. N. Suganthan,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出直觉模糊深度随机向量功能链接网络（IF-dRVFL）和集成版本（IF-edRVFL），通过直觉模糊理论统一处理样本的隶属度与非隶属度，对训练样本赋予自适应权重，从而增强深度随机神经网络在含噪声和离群点数据上的鲁棒性。在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的实验表明所提方法优于现有SOTA模糊与非模糊方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度随机神经网络（如dRVFL和edRVFL）对所有训练样本一视同仁，面对真实世界中含噪声和离群点的数据集时鲁棒性和有效性受限；且污染特征在隐藏层间的传播会负面影响模型的决策能力。

Method: 提出IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL框架，将直觉模糊理论融入深度RVFL网络。具体包括：(1) 在核空间中利用样本邻域信息，同时计算每个样本的隶属度与非隶属度；(2) 隶属度基于样本与其类中心距离计算，非隶属度量化样本在局部邻域内的异质性；(3) 利用这两类度量对训练样本赋予自适应权重，从而区分干净样本、噪声样本和离群点。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上（含/不含高斯噪声）的大量实验表明，所提出的IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL模型在性能上优于现有SOTA的模糊和非模糊方法。

Conclusion: 直觉模糊理论能够有效提升深度随机神经网络的鲁棒性，通过自适应加权机制使模型在含噪和含离群点的真实场景中表现更好。代码已开源。

Abstract: The current state-of-the-art (SOTA) deep randomized neural networks, such as deep Random Vector Functional Link (dRVFL) and ensemble deep RVFL (edRVFL), treat all training samples uniformly, which limits their robustness and effectiveness when applied to real-world datasets containing noise and outliers. Furthermore, the propagation of contaminated features across hidden layers negatively influences the decision-making capability of these models. To overcome these limitations, we propose intuitionistic fuzzy dRVFL (IF-dRVFL) and intuitionistic fuzzy edRVFL (IF-edRVFL) frameworks that enhance model robustness. The proposed models unify intuitionistic fuzzy theory to exploit sample neighborhood information in the kernel space by jointly considering membership and non-membership degrees for each sample. Membership degrees are computed based on the distance of samples from their respective class centroids, while non-membership degrees quantify sample heterogeneity within local neighborhoods. These measures are employed to assign adaptive weights to training samples, enabling effective discrimination among clean, noisy, and outlier data points. Extensive experiments conducted on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, with and without the presence of Gaussian noise, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed IF-dRVFL and IF-edRVFL models over existing SOTA fuzzy and non-fuzzy approaches. The source code is available at https://github.com/mtanveer1/IF-edRVFL.

</details>


### [185] [CurveFP: Rational-Radix Logarithmic Datatypes with Closed Products for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10010)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CurveFP 是一种新型闭乘积码本（closed-product codebook）低精度数据格式，通过交错对数曲线分布量化幅度，使非零乘积在代数上封闭（即乘积仍在码本集合内），从而大幅简化乘积形成路径——只需符号 XOR 和整数索引更新。提供 E4C3/E5C2（8位）用于训练和 E3C3（7位）用于部署，在多个大模型上达到或超越 FP8 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低精度数据格式（如 FP8）主要优化标量保真度（单个数值的表示精度），但未考虑其乘积所引发的算术结构——乘积结果往往不再落在原码本内，导致需要额外舍入或复杂处理。作者希望设计一种数据格式，使量化后的数值乘积天然封闭于码本中，从而简化硬件乘积路径并提升效率。

Method: CurveFP 采用交替交错的对数曲线族来分布量化幅度，并通过紧凑的块级缩放（block scales）进行管理。其核心特征包括：(1) 有理基数（rational radix）在动态范围与局部分辨率之间进行权衡；(2) 均匀曲线索引（uniform curve indices）保证任意非零乘积在代数上封闭——乘积只需符号 XOR 和整数索引更新即可精确构成；(3) 导出的有限相位计数（finite phase count）决定累加调度方案。论文具体实例化为 CurveFP8（E4C3/E5C2）用于训练和 CurveFP7（E3C3）用于紧凑部署。

Result: 在4个7B-9B模型上，CurveFP7 以少一个元素位（7位 vs 8位）超越了逐张量（tensor-wise）FP8 的困惑度，且与原生精度差距仅在1.32%以内。CurveFP8 在全部36对前向和后向 GEMM 比较中降低了操作数 NMSE。在3个匹配的128.3M参数三元组上，所有模式均完成每个种子的3B-token预训练；CurveFP8 达到平均 BF16 推理困惑度22.5366（vs FP8的22.5407），并在全部3个种子中格式引入的惩罚更低。36单元的downstream矩阵中，CurveFP8训练的检查点在全部12个种子-格式比较中取得更低的 WikiText-103 困惑度，PG-19 和任务 delta 表现混合。

Conclusion: CurveFP 作为一种算术协同设计（arithmetic co-design），同时实现了 FP8 级别的数值行为、7位推理精度以及大幅简化的乘积路径，证明对数据格式的乘积代数结构进行显式设计可以在不牺牲精度的前提下显著提升低精度计算的效率与部署能力。

Abstract: Low-precision datatypes reduce language-model cost, but most formats optimize scalar fidelity while leaving the arithmetic induced by their products unchanged. We introduce CurveFP, a closed-product codebook family that distributes quantized magnitudes across interleaved logarithmic curves under compact block scales. A rational radix tunes dynamic range against local resolution, while uniform curve indices make every nonzero product algebraically closed. Product formation becomes an exact sign XOR and integer-index update, and a derived finite phase count determines the accumulation schedule. We instantiate this algebra as CurveFP eight E4C3/E5C2 for training and CurveFP seven E3C3 for compact deployment. In evaluation, CurveFP seven beats tensor-wise FP8 perplexity on four 7B--9B models with one fewer element bit and stays within 1.32\% of native quality. CurveFP eight lowers operand NMSE in all 36 paired forward and backward GEMM comparisons. Across three matched 128.3M-parameter triplets, every mode completes 3B-token pretraining per seed; CurveFP eight reaches mean BF16-inference perplexity 22.5366 versus 22.5407 for FP8 and incurs a lower format-induced penalty in all three seeds. A 36-cell downstream matrix finds lower WikiText-103 perplexity for the CurveFP eight-trained checkpoints in all 12 seed-format comparisons, with mixed PG-19 and task deltas. Together, these results establish CurveFP as an arithmetic co-design that combines FP8-class numerical behavior, seven-bit inference, and a substantially simpler product path.

</details>


### [186] [Sheaf-Based Federated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10016)
*Gabriele D'Acunto,Enrico Grimaldi,Valeria Avino,Mario Edoardo Pandolfo,Leonardo Di Nino,Sergio Barbarossa,Paolo Di Lorenzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SFRL（Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning），一个通用的异构联邦表示学习框架，通过基于可学习sheaf限制映射的流形约束几何对齐正则化器联合优化局部目标，不假设共享全局潜在空间，全局一致性通过正交变换和等距嵌入的邻居潜在表示对齐涌现。配套的Sheaf-FRL算法交替执行局部模型梯度更新和边限制映射的闭式Procrustes更新，并在确定性和随机设置下证明了收敛到一阶驻点。在语义通信的协作分类任务中，SFRL在局部与通信后分类精度及潜在空间维度压缩鲁棒性上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦系统要求智能体在数据分布、感知模态、模型架构、潜在维度和局部学习目标均存在差异的情况下，仍能学习和交换有信息量的表示。现有方法大多假设存在共享的全局潜在空间，这在实际异构场景中往往不成立或难以实现，因此需要一种不依赖共享潜在空间的通用表示学习与对齐框架。

Method: SFRL引入基于sheaf理论的几何对齐框架：将邻居潜在表示通过正交变换和等距嵌入进行对齐，由sheaf Laplacian诱导的二次粘合正则化器施加约束，其中可学习的限制映射根据观测数据自适应调节几何结构。该惩罚项仅在一小组共享pilot样本上评估以保证可扩展性和通信效率。求解算法Sheaf-FRL采用去中心化交替优化：局部模型做梯度更新，边限制映射做闭式Procrustes更新。

Result: 在模型和数据异构的语义通信协作分类任务中，Sheaf-FRL在局部和通信后分类精度上均优于基线方法，在不同程度的局部分布偏移下均表现出优势，并对潜在空间维度压缩展现出更强的鲁棒性。此外，算法在确定性和随机设置下均被证明收敛到一阶驻点。

Conclusion: SFRL证明了无需共享全局潜在空间即可实现异构联邦环境下的全局表示一致性——通过对齐邻居表示而非对齐全局中心，利用sheaf Laplacian驱动的几何正则化实现自适应、可扩展且通信高效的联邦表示学习。Sheaf-FRL在理论与实验层面均验证了其有效性，为异构联邦学习提供了一个全新的几何视角。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated systems require agents to learn and exchange informative representations despite differences in data distributions, sensing modalities, model architectures, latent dimensionalities, and local learning objectives. To address this challenge, we propose Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning (SFRL), a general framework that jointly optimizes local objectives with a manifold-constrained geometric alignment regularizer based on learnable sheaf restriction maps. Unlike most existing approaches, SFRL does not assume a shared global latent space. Instead, global consistency emerges from the alignment of neighboring latent representations through orthogonal transformations and isometric embeddings. This alignment is enforced by a quadratic gluing regularizer induced by the sheaf Laplacian, whose learnable restriction maps adapt the geometry to the observed data. The penalty is evaluated on a small set of shared pilot samples, ensuring scalability and communication efficiency. We develop a decentralized algorithm for solving SFRL, termed Sheaf-FRL, which alternates between gradient updates of the local models and closed-form Procrustes updates of the edge-wise restriction maps. We further establish convergence of Sheaf-FRL to first-order stationary points in both deterministic and stochastic settings. As an application, we consider a cooperative classification task in the context of semantic communication, under model and data heterogeneity. Our results show that Sheaf-FRL outperforms baseline approaches in terms of local and post-communication classification accuracy across different levels of local distribution shift and exhibits greater robustness to latent-space dimensionality compression.

</details>


### [187] [DOCSCHISEL: Adaptive Tool Documentation Optimization Framework for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10037)
*You Lu,Kun Zhang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对LLM智能体的工具文档进行了大规模实证研究，发现不同信息字段的有效性高度依赖于任务领域、LLM骨干和智能体范式，并提出DocsChisel——一个自适应的工具文档优化框架，通过分析失败的执行轨迹来迭代地添加、删除和精炼每个工具的信息字段。实验表明，DocsChisel相比原始文档将任务成功率提升95.89%，相比现有基线平均提升75.15%，且优化时间和token开销有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注提升LLM智能体的工具使用能力，却将工具文档视为固定输入。尽管有少数工作尝试通过重写或压缩优化工具文档，但缺乏对工具文档中不同信息字段如何影响智能体性能的系统性理解，这阻碍了跨不同设置下的通用优化。

Method: 首先进行大规模实证研究，揭示现有工具文档中信息字段的显著异质性以及各字段有效性的情境依赖性。在此基础上提出DocsChisel框架，该框架通过分析目标LLM智能体的失败执行轨迹来识别与文档相关的问题，并针对每个工具通过添加、删除和精炼信息字段进行迭代式文档优化。

Result: DocsChisel在任务成功率上比原始工具文档提升95.89%，比现有最先进基线（EasyTool和DRAFT）平均提升75.15%，同时仅产生有限的优化时间和token开销。

Conclusion: 工具文档不存在放之四海而皆准的最优配置，必须根据具体任务域、LLM骨干和智能体范式进行自适应优化。DocsChisel通过分析失败轨迹实现文档的迭代精细化，验证了自适应工具文档优化在提升LLM智能体性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on external tools to accomplish complex real-world tasks, making tool documentation a critical grounding resource for LLM agents. Existing studies mainly focus on improving the tool-use capabilities of LLM agents, while largely treating tool documentation as a fixed input. Although several recent works attempt to optimize tool documentation through rewriting or compression, little is known about how the information contained in tool documentation affects agent performance across different settings.
  To bridge this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study on tool documentation for LLM agents. Our study reveals substantial heterogeneity in the information fields provided by existing tool documentation. Moreover, the effectiveness of different information fields is highly dependent on the task domain, LLM backbone, and agent paradigm, indicating that no fixed tool documentation can consistently generalize across diverse agent settings.
  Motivated by these findings, we propose DocsChisel, an adaptive tool documentation optimization framework for LLM agents. DocsChisel analyzes failed execution traces of a target LLM agent to identify documentation-related issues, and iteratively optimizes tool documentation by adding, removing, and refining information fields for each tool. We evaluate DocsChisel against two state-of-the-art baselines, i.e., EasyTool and DRAFT. Experimental results show that DocsChisel improves the task success rate of LLM agents by 95.89% over the original tool documentation and by 75.15%, on average, over existing baselines, while incurring limited optimization time and token overhead

</details>


### [188] [FlowScout: From Execution Feedback to Reliable Tool-Using Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10039)
*Shuo Hao,You Lu,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowScout是一个执行引导的框架，用于从历史任务求解记录中自动生成工具集成式的智能体工作流（agentic workflows）。它通过挖掘工具协调骨架构建初始工作流，再利用蒙特卡洛树搜索结合执行反馈来精炼工作流拓扑。在四个任务域上的实验表明，FlowScout在工具调用正确性上比基线至少提升92.69%，在执行质量上至少提升17.66%，且运行稳定性更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体工作流构建主要依赖人工且需要大量领域专业知识；虽然已有研究尝试从历史记录中自动生成工作流，但这些方法主要生成以LLM为中心的工作流，真实工具执行被抽象和模拟为LLM节点，导致生成的工作流可用性和稳定性受限。

Method: FlowScout将智能体工作流表示为由LLM节点、工具调用节点和依赖边组成的有向图。框架首先从历史任务求解记录中挖掘通用的工具协调骨架以构造初始工作流，然后通过蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）并利用执行反馈来精炼工作流拓扑结构。

Result: 在四个代表性任务域上与PM4Py、ReAct和AFlow三个基线对比，FlowScout生成的智能体工作流在工具调用正确性上至少提升92.69%，在执行质量上至少提升17.66%，同时在不同轮次运行中的性能波动更低。

Conclusion: FlowScout通过执行引导的工作流生成方式，有效解决了从历史记录生成工具集成的智能体工作流的问题，显著超越了现有基线方法，并提供了更高的稳定性和可靠性。

Abstract: Agentic workflows have become an important abstraction for building reliable LLM-based automation systems by organizing large language models (LLMs), tools, and control logic into explicit execution structures. However, constructing high-quality agentic workflows remains largely manual and requires substantial domain expertise. Recent studies have explored automatic agentic workflow generation from historical task-solving records, but they mainly produce LLM-centric workflows, where real tool executions are abstracted and simulated by LLM nodes, limiting the usability and stability of generated workflows. To address these limitations, we propose FlowScout, an execution-guided framework for generating tool-integrated agentic workflows from historical task-solving records. Specifically, FlowScout represents an agentic workflow as a directed graph composed of LLM nodes, tool-calling nodes, and dependency edges. It first mines a common tool coordination skeleton from historical records to construct an initial workflow, and then refines the workflow topology through Monte Carlo tree search guided by execution feedback. We evaluate FlowScout on four representative task domains and compare it with three baselines, i.e., PM4Py, ReAct and AFlow. Experimental results show that agentic workflows generated by FlowScout improve tool invocation correctness by at least 92.69% and execution quality by at least 17.66% over the baselines, while achieving lower performance variation across repeated runs.

</details>


### [189] [UserToolBench: A User-Profile-Hidden Benchmark for Personalized Decision Making in Tool-Use LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10042)
*Xuexiong Yin,Zechuan Chen,Yongsen Zheng,Yuxiang Zhang,Jingyuan Yang,Bin Wang,Yubin Wang,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UserToolBench，一个用于评测工具使用LLM个性化决策能力的基准，基于隐私清洗的真实交互轨迹构建，包含10个用户画像、36个工具集、170个工具和1065轮对话，涵盖信息缺失、单工具和多工具三类任务。实验表明现有强模型在个性化委托上仍有困难，尤其是在多工具协调、缺失约束推断和长程行为一致性方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注画像回忆、风格模仿、通用工具使用或响应级个性化，缺乏对工具使用LLM在用户代理场景下的个性化决策能力的系统评估，即模型能否从交互历史中推断潜在用户偏好、识别需要澄清的时机，并在信息不完整的情况下生成符合用户意图的工具调用轨迹。

Method: 从隐私清洗的真实交互轨迹中构建基准，结合结构化人物画像、公共API风格工具生态系统和长程多轮轨迹。包含10个用户画像、36个工具集、1065个回合、170个独特工具，以及三类评测任务：信息缺失场景、单工具场景和多工具场景。

Result: 实验显示强工具使用LLM在个性化委托任务上仍存在明显困难。主要瓶颈包括：多工具协调能力不足、缺失约束推断困难、以及长程行为一致性差。

Conclusion: 个性化评测应超越"输出是否听起来像用户"的标准，转向"LLM是否为所代表的用户做出正确决策"的评估范式。

Abstract: Tool-use LLMs are increasingly asked to act on users' behalf, but existing benchmarks usually focus on profile recall, style imitation, generic tool use, or response-level personalization. We introduce UserToolBench , a benchmark for personalized decision making in tool-use LLMs. UserToolBench tests whether a model can infer latent user preferences from interaction history, recognize when clarification is needed, and produce user-aligned tool-call trajectories under incomplete information. The benchmark is built from privacy-sanitized real interaction traces and combines structured persona profiles, public API-style tool ecosystems, and long-horizon multi-turn trajectories. It includes 10 user profiles, 36 tool sets, 1,065 turns, 170 unique tools, and evaluation-focused task types covering lack-of-information, single-tool, and multi-tool settings. Experiments with strong tool-use LLMs show that current models still have difficulty with personalized delegation. Multi-tool coordination, missing-constraint inference, and long-horizon behavioral consistency remain major bottlenecks. These results suggest that personalization evaluation should move beyond asking whether outputs sound user-specific and instead ask whether LLMs make correct decisions for the users they represent.

</details>


### [190] [SeFoRA: Sketch-Aggregated Federated Low-Rank Adaptation with Heterogeneous Client Ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10144)
*Yue Xia,Tayyebeh Jahani-Nezhad,Mayank Bakshi,Rawad Bitar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SeFoRA，一种基于草图聚合的联邦LoRA算法，通过客户端传输本地更新的线性草图，在联邦服务器端实现直接聚合，缓解了双线性不匹配问题，并证明在秩同质设定下以O(1/T)速率收敛到一阶稳定点邻域，在RoBERTa-Large微调GLUE数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦参数高效微调（PEFT）与LoRA结合时面临两个独特挑战：各客户端可能使用不同的LoRA秩，导致因子矩阵维度不兼容；因子级平均存在双线性不匹配问题（即两个小矩阵乘积的平均不等于平均后的乘积），这两个问题在单独使用联邦学习或LoRA时都不存在。

Method: SeFoRA算法：每个客户端将其本地LoRA更新的线性草图（linear sketch）传输给联邦服务器，使服务器能够在全模型的小子空间中直接聚合更新。这缓解了双线性不匹配问题。同时提出SeFoRA-Ho版本，用于秩同质（rank-homogeneous）场景，支持该设置下适配器的直接聚合。对秩同质设定给出了收敛性分析。

Result: 理论证明在秩同质设定下以O(1/T)速率收敛到一阶稳定点（first-order stationary point）的邻域。实验上在GLUE数据集上微调RoBERTa-Large，结果显示所提算法优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: SeFoRA通过线性草图聚合机制有效解决了联邦LoRA中客户端异质秩与双线性不匹配的核心难题，提供了理论收敛保证，并在大规模语言模型微调任务上验证了其优越性能。

Abstract: We consider federated parameter efficient fine-tuning of large neural networks with low-rank adaptation (LoRA,~Hu et al.\ 2022). Combining LoRA with federated PEFT introduces challenges absent from either setting alone: clients may use different LoRA ranks, making their factor matrices dimension-incompatible, and factor-wise averaging suffers from a bilinear mismatch. We propose SeFoRA, a sketch-aggregated federated LoRA algorithm in which each client transmits a linear sketch of its local updates, enabling direct aggregation at the federator. As a result, SeFoRA alleviates the bilinear mismatch, and allows for aggregation in a small subspace of the full model. We introduce a rank-homogeneous version called SeFoRA-Ho which allows for direct adapter aggregation in this setting. We prove convergence to a neighborhood of the first-order stationary point at rate $\cO(1/T)$ for the rank-homogeneous setting. Numerical experiments on fine-tuning RoBERTa-Large on GLUE datasets show how our algorithms outperform the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [191] [Finding the Signal in the Spam: Jointly Learning Rewards and Worker Reliability from Pairwise Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10045)
*Kaustubh Shivshankar Shejole,Tanish Agarwal,Arpit Agarwal,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从成对比较中联合学习物品奖励和众包工人能力（可靠性）的问题。作者采用Boltzmann-rational模型（即引入工人能力的Bradley-Terry-Luce扩展模型），通过引入Polya-Gamma潜变量将逻辑似然转化为条件高斯形式，推导出基于EM的算法，并将其转化为矩阵感知问题以建立理论收敛保证。实验表明该方法在真实和合成数据上优于多个基线，对垃圾信息和对抗性工人具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对比较学习广泛应用于推荐系统、社会选择和大型语言模型微调等领域。然而，众包平台上工人往往不可靠，可能因领域知识有限或追求收入最大化而随意回答（垃圾信息/作弊）。现有方法通常假设工人完全可靠或未能同时考虑工人能力和物品奖励的学习，因此需要一种能联合建模和推断工人能力与物品奖励的鲁棒方法。

Method: 采用Boltzmann-rational模型，将BTL模型扩展为包含工人能力参数的形式。通过引入Polya-Gamma潜变量将Logistic似然转换为条件高斯形式，使期望最大化（EM）算法中的E步Q函数得以简化，进而将问题归约为矩阵感知问题。基于此矩阵感知框架，建立算法的理论收敛保证。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上进行了广泛实验，证明所提算法优于多个基线方法，并展现出对垃圾信息工人（spammers）和对抗性工人的强鲁棒性，验证了算法在现实众包和奖励学习场景中的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明工人能力可以从成对比较数据中与物品奖励联合学习，所提出的基于Polya-Gamma潜变量的EM算法在理论和实验层面均表现出良好性能，为不可靠众包环境下的奖励学习提供了实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: The problem of learning from pairwise comparisons has been widely studied across many domains such as recommendation systems, social choice, and more recently, fine-tuning large language models. In this problem, the goal is to learn item rewards based on pairwise comparisons between them. In many scenarios, these comparisons are elicited from crowdworkers using platforms such as Amazon Mechanical Turk, Scale AI, etc. However, crowdworkers are often unreliable due to limited domain knowledge or revenue-maximizing (spamming) behavior. In this work, our goal is to understand whether worker reliability (competency) can be learned jointly with item rewards. To this end, we adopt the Boltzmann-rational model for pairwise comparisons, which extends the Bradley-Terry-Luce model by incorporating worker competencies. We derive an EM-based algorithm for learning under this model by introducing Polya-Gamma latent variables to transform the logistic likelihood into a conditionally Gaussian form, enabling tractable optimization and leading to a simplified $Q$ function in the E-step of the algorithm. This technique allows us to reduce our formulation to a matrix sensing problem, using which we establish theoretical convergence guarantees for our algorithm. We conduct extensive experiments on real-world and synthetic datasets. These experiments demonstrate the advantages of using our algorithm over several baselines and confirm its strong robustness to both spammers and adversarial workers, highlighting its practical effectiveness in realistic crowdsourcing and reward learning settings. The code and data is publicly available at https://github.com/KaustubhShejole/BoRa_EM.

</details>


### [192] [TideRL: Boosting Agentic RL Goodput with Readiness-Aware Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10402)
*Yanyu Ren,Xizheng Wang,Xiao Liu,Bowen Lv,Hanchen Zhang,Shudan Zhang,Hanyu Lai,Shuai Wang,Li Chen,Dan Li,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TideRL是一个针对多轮智能体工作负载的就绪感知弹性强化学习系统，通过连续任务批处理、资源感知的Ref-Actor流水线和弹性资源扩展三大机制，将RL训练goodput提升最高5.6倍（相对同步基线）和33%以上（相对异步基线），同时将KV缓存命中率提升1.58倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的强化学习正转向多轮智能体工作负载，其中rollout任务会反复暂停等待外部环境、以不断增长的上下文恢复、并在高度可变的时间完成。在这种场景下，RL训练的实际有效吞吐（goodput）比原始GPU占用率更重要，因为GPU等待和重复的prefill重计算都是纯粹的额外开销。

Method: TideRL包含三个核心组件：1) CTB（连续任务批处理），保留有价值的rollout状态；2) RA²P（资源感知的Ref-Actor流水线），根据就绪积压量和到达间隔在解耦流式与同址聚合之间选择；3) ERS（弹性资源扩展），利用相同的就绪信号在rollout与训练之间移动rank。

Result: 在纯文本和多模态智能体工作负载上，TideRL将RL训练goodput提升最高5.6倍（相对同步基线）、33%以上（相对异步基线），同时达到相似的任务性能。此外，KV缓存命中率提升1.58倍，每步训练时间最多减少44.3%，总等待时间最多减少77.6%。

Conclusion: TideRL通过就绪感知的弹性调度策略，有效解决了多轮智能体RL训练中因任务到达时间高度可变而导致的GPU等待和prefill重计算开销问题，在保持任务性能的同时显著提升了训练效率和资源利用率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models is moving toward multi-turn agentic workloads, where rollout tasks repeatedly pause for external environments, resume with growing contexts, and finish at highly variable times. In this setting, RL training goodput, measured by training throughput, matters more than raw GPU occupancy: GPU waiting and repeated prefill recomputation are pure overhead. We present TideRL, a readiness-aware elastic RL system with Continuous Task Batching, Resource-Aware Ref-Actor Pipelining, and Elastic Resource Scaling. CTB preserves useful rollout state, $\textrm{RA}^2\textrm{P}$ selects between decoupled streaming and colocated aggregation from the ready backlog and arrival interval, and ERS moves ranks between rollout and training using the same readiness signals. Across text-only and multi-modal agentic workloads, TideRL improves RL training goodput by up to 5.6$\times$ over synchronous baselines and over 33% over asynchronous baselines, while reaching similar task performance. It also improves KV cache hit rate by 1.58$\times$, reduces per-step training time by up to 44.3%, and cuts total waiting time by up to 77.6%.

</details>


### [193] [Detecting Soft Skills in ML Engineering Roles CVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10046)
*Aidin Azamnouri,Nouran Ayad,Justus Bogner,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从求职者供给侧研究ML工程师、数据科学家、软件工程师的软技能表述，利用300份人工精选CV和LLM流水线提取显式列表与隐式叙事软技能，将需求侧文献的论断转化为13个可证伪假设并加以检验，其中11个成立、1个部分成立、1个被推翻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于ML系统构建团队软技能的知识几乎全部来自需求侧（招聘广告、调查、面试官访谈），捕捉的是雇主要求；而求职者如何表述自身能力从未被研究，且现有CV挖掘工作存在两大缺陷：一是基于关键词无法识别叙事性传达的技能，二是仅做描述性频率排序、未检验群体差异是否超过抽样误差。

Method: 构建包含三种角色的300份平衡精选CV语料库，使用经人工标注真值验证的LLM流水线，区分显式列表与隐性叙事的软技能提取（这是现有提取器未设计的功能）；将需求侧文献论断转化为13个关于角色特征、资历进阶和披露风格的假说，采用效应量并结合族系错误率控制进行统计检验。

Result: 11个假说获支持、1个部分支持、1个被推翻。求职者以叙事而非关键词列表方式披露软技能约为3:1，在雇主最重视的能力（领导力、协调、指导）上叙事占比最高（88-96%）。资历提升将表达领导力的几率提升近3倍。此前被视为普遍能力的领导力，软件工程师的表达率仅为同行一半。技术型求职者确实会表达软技能，但基于关键词的筛选会系统性地遗漏它们。

Conclusion: 求职者侧的实证数据可以证实或反驳需求侧叙事，而非仅仅加以例证；同时揭示了现有基于关键词的简历筛选方法在识别软技能方面的系统性盲区，对招聘实践有直接启发意义。

Abstract: Soft skills shape collaboration among ML engineers, data scientists, and software engineers building ML-enabled systems, yet what we know about them comes almost entirely from the demand side. Job advertisements, surveys, and hiring manager interviews capture what employers ask for. How candidates themselves articulate these competencies has not been studied, and existing CV-mining work is both keyword-based, so it cannot see skills conveyed through narrative, and descriptive, reporting frequency rankings without testing whether group differences exceed sampling variation. We close both gaps. Using a balanced corpus of 300 curated CVs spanning the three roles, we extract explicitly listed and implicitly narrated soft skills with an LLM-based pipeline validated against a human-annotated ground truth, a distinction that existing extractors were not designed to make. We then convert the demand-side literature's claims into 13 falsifiable hypotheses about role signatures, seniority progression, and disclosure style, and test them with effect sizes under family-wise error control, so that candidate-side data can corroborate or contradict the demand-side account rather than merely illustrate it. Eleven hypotheses are supported, one partially, and one refuted. Candidates disclose soft skills through narrative rather than keyword lists by roughly three to one, and most so for the competencies employers value most: leadership, coordination, and mentoring (88-96% narrative). Seniority nearly triples the odds of articulating leadership. That competency, assumed universal in prior work, is articulated by software engineers at half the rate of their peers. Technical candidates do articulate soft skills, but a keyword-based screening systematically misses them.

</details>


### [194] [Physics-Informed Machine Learning in Prognostics and Health Management: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10047)
*Christopher Braun,Julian Raible,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述（212项研究）探讨物理信息机器学习（PIML）在设备健康管理（PHM）中的应用，提出四类分类体系（观测偏置、归纳偏置、学习偏置和混合方法），发现PIML方法在预测性能上普遍优于传统基线，但文献偏向锂离子电池和轴承，且关于泛化性、因果推断和可解释性等优势的宣称缺乏充分证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的机器学习模型在PHM中面临固有局限，包括泛化能力差、无法推断因果关系以及缺乏可解释性。PIML通过将先验物理知识融入机器学习流程来缓解这些问题，因此有必要系统梳理PIML在PHM领域的应用现状与研究进展。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法，纳入212项研究；提出包含观测偏置（observational bias）、归纳偏置（inductive bias）、学习偏置（learning bias）和混合方法（hybrid）的四类分类方案，并按PHM任务（如诊断、预测）进一步归类，对各类方法的性能提升和局限进行分析。

Result: 四类PIML方法在多种资产上均一致表现出优于传统基线的预测性能；但文献高度集中于锂离子电池和轴承领域，且以问题特定解决方案为主导；关于PIML在泛化性、因果推断与可解释性等方面的改进声称缺乏足够的支持证据。

Conclusion: PIML已在PHM中展现切实收益，但未来研究应优先关注可迁移的设计模式、集成策略的基准对比，以及开发轻量、鲁棒且适用于在线部署场景的不确定性感知模型。

Abstract: In modern industry, keeping complex systems reliable, safe, and efficient hinges on Prognostics and Health Management (PHM). Machine Learning (ML) has largely driven advancements in diagnostics and prognostics, yet purely data-driven models face inherent limitations, such as poor generalization, an inability to infer causal relationships, and a lack of interpretability. Physics-Informed Machine Learning (PIML) helps mitigate these limitations by incorporating prior physical knowledge directly into the ML pipeline, thereby fostering growing interest in its application to PHM. This work investigates how PIML is being leveraged in the context of PHM through a systematic literature review of 212 studies. The review introduces a four-class classification scheme, consisting of observational bias, inductive bias, learning bias, and hybrid approaches, and further categorizes studies by PHM task. Across all four classes, the reviewed studies consistently demonstrate improved predictive performance over conventional baselines across a broad range of assets, although the literature is heavily skewed toward lithium-ion batteries and bearings, and dominated by problem-specific solutions. Overall, the review indicates that physics-informed approaches already provide tangible benefits, whereas claims of improvements concerning some of the aforementioned limitations lack sufficient supporting evidence. Future research should prioritize transferable design patterns, benchmarks comparing integration strategies, and uncertainty-aware models that are lightweight and robust enough for online deployment in real-world settings.

</details>


### [195] [Observational Policy Ranking for SMB Financial Guidance from Multi-Action Accounting Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10050)
*Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam,Vikas Raturi,Kamalika Das,Xiang Gao,Kratika Gupta,Ruocheng Guo,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Ananya Pramod,Sricharan Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出协变量调整残差策略学习（CAR-PL），基于85,078个公司月观测数据（7,505家企业），从多热编码的会计日志中为中小企业选择34种业务变更类别以优化目标财务KPI。在多个基线模型对比中，CAR-PL在毛利上表现最佳（0.084），T-Learner在收入上表现最佳（0.085），上下文价值模型在速动比率上表现最佳（0.062），且CAR-PL选择更分散的类别组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业需要及时的财务指导，但历史会计日志记录的是自我选择且常共现的业务变更，而非随机化的建议。这使得传统的策略学习难以直接适用，需要从观测数据中学习方法来评估和排序不同业务变更类别对财务KPI的效果。

Method: 提出CAR-PL（Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning），一种基于action-wise R-learner的方法，直接在多热编码日志上操作，并通过观测支持（observational support）进行正则化。与T-Learner、保守上下文价值模型、零样本LLM及非个性化参考模型在公司不相交的保留集上，采用共享的模型辅助评分规则进行比较。

Result: CAR-PL在毛利上取得最高点估计值（0.084），T-Learner在收入上最高（0.085），上下文价值模型在速动比率上最高（0.062）。在两个增长KPI上，CAR-PL和T-Learner在公司聚类比较中无统计学显著差异，但CAR-PL选择了33-34个类别且选择分布更分散。在仅用结果模型评分及替换零治疗参考为最常见训练共现模式时，排名保持相似。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，从多动作会计日志中可以针对特定目标KPI进行客观排序的中小企业财务指导，CAR-PL在此设定下提供了稳健且选择多样化的策略学习方法。

Abstract: Small and medium-sized businesses need timely financial guidance, yet historical accounting logs record self-selected and often co-occurring business changes rather than randomized recommendations. We formulate this setting as observational policy ranking: from pre-decision financial information, a policy selects one of 34 ledger-derived business-change categories for a target financial KPI. Using 85,078 company-month observations from 7,505 firms, we introduce Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning (CAR-PL), an action-wise R-learner that operates directly on multi-hot logs and regularizes selection by observational support. We compare CAR-PL with an uplift T-Learner, a conservative contextual value model, a zero-shot LLM, and non-personalized references on company-disjoint held-out firms under a shared model-assisted scoring rule. CAR-PL has the highest Gross Profit point estimate (0.084), the T-Learner has the highest Revenue point estimate (0.085), and the contextual value model has the highest Quick Ratio point estimate (0.062). CAR-PL and the T-Learner are not statistically separated on either growth KPI in matched company-clustered comparisons, while CAR-PL selects 33-34 categories and produces less concentrated selections across the catalog. Outcome-model-only scoring retains the same KPI-level point-estimate leader or top pair, and category rankings remain similar when the all-zero treatment reference is replaced by the most common training co-action pattern. These findings support objective-specific ranking of SMB financial guidance from multi-action accounting logs.

</details>


### [196] [ChronoSSM: Training for Temporally Aware Representations in Autoregressive State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10120)
*Adrien Schoen,Nachiketa Ratnakar Patil,Arjun Bhagoji,Francesco Bronzino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ChronoSSM，一种联合建模事件与时间戳的自回归状态空间模型，通过共享骨干和联合生成目标训练，证明联合训练比两阶段训练能产生更具时间信息性的表征而不损害事件建模质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型（从Transformer到SSM）训练时主要关注事件预测（何时发生被当作次要问题），在具有显式时间信息的场景中，这种分离限制了时间推理、异常检测和时间线重建。常见的两阶段策略（先用事件预测学习表征，再用冻结表征训练时间模型）隐含假设事件预测的表征已包含足够的时序结构，这一假设可能不成立。

Method: ChronoSSM是一个自回归状态空间模型（SSM），通过共享骨干网络联合建模事件和时间戳，使用组合的令牌生成和时间生成目标进行训练。论文对比了联合训练（时间监督更新骨干网络）与两阶段训练（时序仅在冻结的事件表征上学习）两种模式。

Result: 在四个领域（覆盖密集和部分时间戳监督）中，联合训练一致地使冻结表征中的到达间隔信息更可恢复，同时没有在内容生成质量上造成系统性退化。结果表明时间监督可以产生更具时间信息性的表征，而不会实质性地损害自回归事件建模。

Conclusion: 时间监督的联合训练能够在不牺牲事件预测质量的前提下显著提升表征的时间信息性，验证了共享骨干联合建模的有效性，为带时间戳的序列数据挖掘提供了更鲁棒的表示学习方案。

Abstract: Modern sequence models, from Transformers to State Space Models, have enabled powerful generative modeling across diverse domains, yet they are typically trained to predict what happens while treating when it happens as a secondary concern. In data-mining settings where events are associated with explicit timing information, this separation can limit temporal reasoning, anomaly detection, and faithful reconstruction of event chronology. A common strategy is to treat timing as an auxiliary signal, training a separate timing model using representations learned solely for event prediction. However, this two-stage approach implicitly assumes that representations optimized for event prediction already contain sufficient temporal structure.
  We introduce ChronoSSM, an autoregressive State Space Model (SSM) that jointly models events and timestamps with a shared backbone trained using combined token and temporal generation objectives. We compare the joint regime, where temporal supervision updates the backbone, with the two-stage regime, where timing is learned only using the frozen event representations. Across four domains spanning dense and partial timestamp supervision, joint training consistently makes inter-arrival information more recoverable from frozen representations without any systematic degradation in content-generation quality overall. Our results show that temporal supervision can produce more temporally informative representations without materially degrading autoregressive event modeling.

</details>


### [197] [Procedural Fairness Failures in RLHF from Preference Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10126)
*M P V S Gopinadh,Karthik Kamuju,Kummari Avinash,John Joshua,Srinivasa Raju Rudraraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有RLHF假设偏好同质，将异质偏好聚合为单一奖励模型会导致程序公平性失败（多数群体主导、少数群体偏好被系统性低估）。作者提出PA-RLHF，在奖励学习阶段跨偏好模式分离优化，在受控实验中将对齐准确率从46.9%提升至67.9%，并将最优/最差对齐组之间的公平差距从15.9缩小至9.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF将异构的人类偏好聚合为单一奖励模型，隐含假设偏好同质性。当偏好真实异质时，这种聚合导致多数群体偏好主导奖励学习，少数群体偏好被系统性代表不足，构成程序公平性失败。

Method: 定义对齐中的程序公平性为奖励建模阶段保留不同偏好信号，指出标准RLHF通过偏好平均化违反该原则；提出PA-RLHF，在奖励学习阶段按偏好模式分离优化路径，使不同偏好群体各自得到独立建模而非被平均化。

Result: 在受控（无噪声）环境中，PA-RLHF将对齐准确率从46.9%提升至67.9%，并将最优与最差对齐群体间的公平差距从15.9个百分点缩小至9.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 对齐中的程序公平性失败可源于奖励学习的结构性设计选择，即使在受控、无噪声环境中也会发生；对LLM和智能体系统具有直接影响，因为偏置的奖励模型会在顺序决策中加剧不公平性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) aggregates heterogeneous preferences into a single reward model, assuming preference homogeneity. When preferences are heterogeneous, this aggregation induces a procedural fairness failure where majority preference groups dominate reward learning while minority preferences are systematically under-represented. This work defines procedural fairness in alignment as preserving distinct preference signals during reward modeling and shows that standard RLHF violates this via preference averaging. Preference-Aware RLHF (PA-RLHF) is introduced, separating optimization across preference modes at the reward learning stage. In a controlled setting, PA-RLHF improves overall alignment accuracy from 46.9% to 67.9% and reduces the fairness gap between best and worst aligned groups from 15.9 to 9.6 percentage points. These results show that procedural fairness failures in alignment can arise from structural design choices in reward learning, even in controlled, noise-free settings, with direct implications for large language models and agentic systems, where biased reward models can compound inequities across sequential decisions.

</details>


### [198] [The Evaluation Protocol Determines the Result: An Independent Reproduction of LeWorldModel on TwoRoom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10145)
*Joyjeet Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对LeWorldModel进行独立复现，验证了其约87%的目标达成率，但在复现过程中发现四个决定结果但未出现在任何配置文件中的关键约定。同时证明评估协议本身存在争议（不同的目标偏移和步骤预算可导致14%到84%的结果差异），并揭示两个普遍发现：一步预测精度不能预测长时域规划成功，以及批归一化层会使验证损失膨胀至300倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LeWorldModel论文声称的87%目标达成率是否可复现，并揭示原始论文中未披露的关键实现细节和评估协议问题，从而评估该研究的可靠性和泛化性。

Method: 独立重新实现LeWorldModel，使用约25美元租用计算资源和笔记本CPU进行全部评估；系统地改变评估协议参数（目标偏移、步骤预算、目标构建方式）来检验结果的稳健性；跨三个检查点比较一步预测精度与长时域规划成功之间的关系；分析批归一化层对验证损失的影响。

Result: 独立复现达到94.0%目标达成率（超过作者声称的87%），位置探针Pearson r=0.9988（与报告的0.996一致）。但发现四个关键约定未在配置文件中披露。评估协议对结果影响巨大：不同目标偏移和步骤预算下，作者权重仅达14.0%和84.0%，仅配置中的值能复现报告结果；改变目标构建方式使结果从84.0%骤降至8.0%。一步预测精度不能预测长时域规划成功；批归一化使验证损失膨胀高达300倍。

Conclusion: LeWorldModel的基本结果可复现，但依赖于未披露的实现细节和脆弱的评估协议。论文的核心评价指标（预测损失）与实际能力（规划成功）之间缺乏关联，且验证协议对细微改动高度敏感，表明该研究结果的稳健性和可推广性存在问题。

Abstract: LeWorldModel trains a latent world model with a prediction loss and a single anti-collapse regulariser, and reports approximately 87% of goals reached on TwoRoom, its simplest diagnostic environment. We reproduce that result by independent reimplementation on roughly $25 of rented compute, with all evaluation on one laptop CPU.
  We reach 94.0% at the repository's evaluation goal offset, against 84.0% for the authors' own released checkpoint measured under our protocol on identical episodes, and we reproduce the reported representation result directly (position probe Pearson r = 0.9988 against a reported 0.996). Reaching that point required four conventions that determine the outcome and appear in no released configuration file: dense action gathering across a frameskip block, a programmatically-set action-encoder width, ImageNet pixel normalisation, and action z-scoring. A reproducer following the released configurations alone obtains a model whose predictor cannot converge.
  The evaluation protocol is itself contested by the released material. The paper's appendix and the repository's configuration specify different goal offsets and step budgets; on the authors' own weights these yield 14.0% and 84.0%, and only the configuration's values reproduce the reported figure. On fifty identical episodes, changing nothing but how the goal is constructed moves that checkpoint from 84.0% to 8.0%.
  Two findings generalise. One-step prediction accuracy does not predict long-horizon planning success: across three checkpoints spanning a sevenfold range in prediction error, including the authors' own, it orders short-horizon success monotonically and fails to order long-horizon success at all. And a batch normalisation layer inflated our reported validation loss by up to a factor of 300, concealing a training loss that was flat throughout.

</details>


### [199] [REATS: LLM Reasoning-based Ensemble Learning for Adaptive Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10149)
*Xu Zhang,Chang Xu,Hui Sun,Nan Ma,Zijian Zhang,Peng Wang,Wei Wang,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REATS框架，利用大语言模型的推理能力作为智能集成路由器，通过链式思维推理联合处理文本时间序列模式描述和数值特征，生成可解释、样本自适应的集成权重，在八个基准上超越现有集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中时间序列的多样性导致单一预测模型难以在所有样本上表现一致。现有集成学习方法依赖固定规则或仅基于数值输入的黑盒模型，未能利用LLM的推理能力进行可解释的权重决策。

Method: (i) 结构化输入管道：将原始时间序列转换为混合文本-数值表示，固定token成本，实现无API依赖的规则化链式思维构建，并结合检索到的相似样本先验；(ii) 多样多行权重监督方案配合token高效的百分比表格格式，降低数值复杂度、缓解LLM幻觉；(iii) 两阶段微调框架（SFT+GRPO），通过倒数奖励映射将连续无界MSE差转换为有界信号并放大近最优敏感性，解决朴素奖励设计中均匀敏感性和异常值主导的优势压缩问题。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上，REATS超越竞争性集成基线，同时提供自然语言解释，并展现出对未见候选模型的强迁移学习和域外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 利用LLM推理进行可解释的样本自适应集成加权是有效的，配套的设计选择（结构化输入、多样权重监督、两阶段微调）能够显著提升集成性能并支持跨模型泛化。

Abstract: Due to the diversity of real-world time series, no single forecasting model consistently dominates across all samples. Ensemble learning addresses this by combining complementary model strengths, yet existing methods rely on fixed rules or black-box models based solely on numerical inputs, failing to leverage LLM reasoning for interpretable weighting decisions. We propose REATS, which leverages LLM reasoning capabilities as an intelligent ensemble router that jointly processes textual temporal pattern descriptions and numerical features to produce interpretable, sample-adaptive ensemble weights through chain-of-thought reasoning. To enable effective LLM-based ensembling, we study its key design choices and propose: (i) a structured input pipeline that transforms raw time series into hybrid textual--numerical representations with fixed token cost, enabling rule-based chain-of-thought construction without API dependency, augmented with retrieved similar-sample priors; (ii) a diverse multi-row weight supervision scheme coupled with a token-efficient percentage-table format that reduces numerical complexity and mitigates LLM hallucinations; and (iii) a two-stage fine-tuning framework combining SFT with GRPO, where a reciprocal reward mapping transforms the continuous unbounded MSE gap into bounded signals with amplified near-oracle sensitivity, addressing the uniform sensitivity and outlier-dominated advantage compression inherent in naive reward designs for regression-based GRPO. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate that REATS outperforms competitive ensemble baselines while providing natural language explanations and demonstrating strong transfer learning and out-of-domain generalization to unseen candidate models.

</details>


### [200] [Intrinsic Structure: Spectral Identifiability for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10172)
*Ashim Dhor,Pin-Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Koopman算子将前向传播视为受控动力系统，从而为机械可解释性的字典学习建立可辨识性（identifiability）理论基础。作者证明模型谱（spectrum）是坐标无关的内在属性，可从M个校准样本中以M^{-1/2}速率（至多排列意义下）恢复，并给出匹配的极小极大下界、重尾激活的中位数均值变体以及解离定理（可辨识对象与可理解对象不同）。在GPT-2 small、Gemma-2-2B和Qwen3-8B-Base上谱收敛，实验支持理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性虽然能通过识别电路来解释模型，但无法判断一个电路是模型的固有属性还是发现方法的人为产物。稀疏自编码器尤其暴露此问题：不同种子和宽度从相同激活中恢复出实质性不同的特征，而现有理论无法说明这种变异性是偶然的还是结构性的。作者希望将字典学习置于可辨识性的严格基础上。

Method: 将前向传播视为以深度为时间的受控动力系统，通过Koopman算子将其提升为有限线性实现，其谱是模型的坐标无关属性。理论部分证明该谱可从M个校准样本以M^{-1/2}速率恢复（至排列意义），给出匹配极小极大下界、重尾激活的中位数均值变体，以及解离定理：当实现非正规时，携带激活方差的与携带跨深度信息的方向不可重合。

Result: 在GPT-2 small、Gemma-2-2B和Qwen3-8B-Base上谱处处收敛，Qwen3-8B-Base上达到预测的指数0.506±0.031；短落在各cell样本阈值上塌缩为一条曲线。Koopman模式在间接宾语识别上优于随机方向但逊于主成分，差距按深度距离以4.1倍衰减，与定理预测一致。

Conclusion: Koopman谱是一种可辨识的、模型内在的指纹，带有明确的误差条，但它不是一种可读（legible）的分解。可辨识的对象与可理解的对象不是同一个对象。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability explains models by identifying circuits inside them, but has no way to tell whether a circuit is a property of the model or an artifact of the method that found it. Sparse autoencoders illustrate the problem: different seeds and widths recover materially different features from the same activations, and no theory says whether that variability is incidental or structural. We put dictionary learning for interpretability on an identifiability footing. Treating the forward pass as a controlled dynamical system with depth as time and lifting it with the Koopman operator yields a finite linear realisation whose \emph{spectrum} is a coordinate-free property of the model. We prove the spectrum is recoverable from $M$ calibration samples at rate $M^{-1/2}$ up to permutation - to our knowledge the first identifiability theorem for a mechanistic-interpretability primitive, with a matching minimax lower bound, a median-of-means variant for heavy-tailed activations, and a dissociation theorem: whenever the realisation is non-normal, the directions carrying activation variance and the directions carrying information across depth cannot coincide. The identifiable object and the legible object are not the same object. On GPT-2 small, Gemma-2-2B and Qwen3-8B-Base the spectrum converges everywhere and attains the predicted exponent on Qwen3-8B-Base ($0.506 \pm 0.031$); shortfalls collapse onto one curve against each cell's sample threshold. Koopman modes beat random directions but lose to principal components on indirect-object identification, with the gap decaying $4.1\times$ in depth-distance, as the theorem predicts. The Koopman spectrum is an identifiable, model-intrinsic fingerprint with a stated error bar, not a legible decomposition.

</details>


### [201] [From Prediction to Incrementality: Causal Optimization for Large-Scale Targeting and Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10182)
*Changshuai Wei,John Bencina,Phuc Nguyen,Andre Assuncao Silva T Ribeiro,Benjamin Zelditch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个决策中心化的因果优化框架，将因果神经网络（Transformer骨干）、贝叶斯神经bandit层和基于对偶的大规模线性规划分配层统一到单一目标下，用于在全局业务约束下优化增量因果效应。在LinkedIn Feed营销流量上的在线A/B测试中实现了统计显著的+7.20%长期价值指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模定向和推荐系统通常围绕预测分数配合启发式或局部分配构建，但当业务目标是增量影响（如营销活动、激励和通知）时，该范式系统性地将资源错误分配给那些无论如何都会行动的用户，导致资源错配。

Method: 构建一个决策中心化框架，将三个组件在单一目标下对齐：(1) 基于Transformer骨干的因果神经网络用于个体治疗效果估计；(2) 贝叶斯神经bandit层用于不确定性感知的探索；(3) 基于对偶的大规模线性规划层用于带约束的分配。框架还通过Transformer编码器和结果嵌入支持序列上下文、多结果和属性条件评分。

Result: 在公开bandit数据集上的离线仿真、针对性的架构消融实验以及LinkedIn Feed营销流量的在线A/B测试中进行了评估。端到端治疗策略在主要长期价值指标上实现了统计显著的+7.20%提升。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在业务约束下进行生产级规模因果优化的可行性，并提炼了关于因果训练数据构建及成本与交付控制的生产经验，这些对成功部署至关重要。

Abstract: Large-scale targeting and recommendation systems are typically built around predictive scores fed into heuristic or local allocation. When the business goal is incremental impact, as in marketing campaigns, incentives, and notifications, this paradigm systematically misallocates resources toward users who would have acted anyway. We present a decision-centric framework that instead optimizes causal effects under global constraints, aligning three components under a single objective: a causal neural network with a Transformer backbone for individual treatment-effect estimation, a Bayesian neural-bandit layer for uncertainty-aware exploration, and a dual-based large-scale linear-programming layer for constrained allocation. The framework also supports sequential context and multi-outcome, attribute-conditioned scoring through a Transformer encoder and outcome embeddings. We evaluate it with offline simulations on a public bandit dataset, targeted architectural ablations, and an online A/B test on LinkedIn Feed marketing traffic. We also distill production lessons on causal training-data construction and cost and delivery control, which were critical to successful deployment. The end-to-end treatment policy delivered a statistically significant $+7.20\%$ lift in the primary long-term-value metric, demonstrating the feasibility of production-scale causal optimization under business constraints.

</details>


### [202] [ELMER: Evolutionary Language Model that Explores and Refines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10196)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种进化语言模型，在自然语言策略描述空间中搜索并编译为可执行程序，通过条件语义突变和直接偏好优化（oDPO）实现行为空间中可控的进化搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序进化能衡量突变是否有效，但很少控制突变在行为空间中移动的距离。语法编辑大小不可靠：小的代码改动可能改变几乎所有行为，而大的重写可能保持相同的执行轨迹。因此需要一种可控制突变强度、在行为空间中可校准的进化搜索机制。

Method: 提出Evolutionary Language Model (ELM)，在自然语言策略描述上搜索，并编译为类型化程序执行。使用全量微调的Qwen3-8B模型学习三种任务条件操作：条件语义突变、自然语言到领域特定语言GPTL的编译、以及GPTL到自然语言的翻译。模型以突变强度（低/中/高）为条件输入，使用oDPO（直接偏好优化）进行微调。

Result: 在252个固定预算进化搜索中，oDPO同时提升了行为校准和有限预算搜索效率。自然语言达到了最高的观测适应度。条件输入（突变强度）系统性地改变了语义编辑组成，语言突变在匹配的小到中度行为位移下保留了更多父代适应度。

Conclusion: 语言可以作为可操控的、基于执行落地的搜索表示，在可执行程序空间中进行可控的进化搜索，克服了语法编辑大小作为行为位移代理不可靠的问题。

Abstract: Program evolution can measure whether a mutation helped, but it rarely controls how far the mutation moves in behavior space. Syntactic edit size is an unreliable proxy: a small code change can alter nearly every action, while a larger rewrite can preserve the same execution trace.
