Abstract: Skill abstraction---the process of learning reusable and temporally extended behaviors---has emerged as a key paradigm for improving sample efficiency and generalization in robot learning. For efficient skill transfer to real robots, learned skills must generalize across tasks and domains, remain robust to visual and dynamic perturbations, and be efficient enough for practical deployment. However, existing methods typically satisfy only a subset of these properties, as they capture either high-level semantic intent (what) or low-level motion dynamics (how). This incomplete skill transfer yields weak priors for policy learning, thereby demanding substantial in-domain data for downstream adaptation. To address these challenges, we introduce BooST, a two-stage framework that explicitly bridges semantics and motions to satisfy all three desiderata. BooST first leverages a cross-modal VQ-VAE to capture both semantic intent and motion dynamics, yielding a unified skill representation. It then distills this representation into a lightweight policy for efficient downstream adaptation to new tasks. Extensive experiments across simulation and real-robot settings demonstrate that BooST achieves superior few-shot adaptation, cross-domain skill transfer, and robustness to dynamic visual distractors, while maintaining a lightweight yet expressive design suitable for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [371] [Toward the Cognitive--Physical Limits of Embodied Intelligence through a World-Model-Centric Autonomous Racing Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10618)
*Zitong Shan,Baichuan Lou,Yanxin Zhou,Shuge Wu,Xianqi He,Bolin Zhao,Sheng Zhao,Zhouheng Li,Chee Kiong Ong,King Ho Holden Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以世界模型为中心的自动驾驶赛车智能体，通过从接近极限的成功与失败中学习预测性世界模型，耦合世界状态构建、前瞻推理和近极限控制，在闭环迭代中持续精化智能体的认知-物理能力边界。实车最高速度256.3 km/h、峰值横向加速度26.8 m/s²的数据支持下，智能体在多样仿真赛车场景中达到88.3%的交互成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能系统大多在保守安全裕度或中等交互范围内评估，极端条件下能力边界理解不足。自动驾驶赛车提供高频率定位感知、对抗交互、近饱和动力学和严格安全约束的严苛测试平台，但现有系统很少联合建模与精化认知和物理极限。

Method: 框架学习预测性世界模型，从近极限的成功与失败中捕捉交互演化、自我动力学和可行运动边界；通过闭环精化过程耦合世界状态构建、未来感知推理和近极限控制，训练数据来自真实车辆自动驾驶赛车场景。

Result: 实车系统在高达256.3 km/h速度和26.8 m/s²峰值横向加速度下保持稳健定位与感知；全尺寸仿真赛车中智能体在多样挑战场景下达到88.3%交互成功率。闭环精化进一步提升了认知-物理极限利用率、失败模式恢复能力及跨条件和未见赛道的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 提出一种边界感知方法论，世界模型帮助具身智能体表示、预测并持续精化能力边界，为更安全的真实世界部署提供有力支撑。

Abstract: Embodied artificial intelligence aims to develop agents that perceive, reason, and act through continuous interaction with the physical world. However, most embodied systems are still evaluated within conservative safety margins or moderate interaction regimes, leaving their capability boundaries under extreme conditions insufficiently understood. Autonomous racing provides a stringent testbed by combining high-frequency localization and perception, adversarial interaction, near-saturated vehicle dynamics, and strict safety constraints. Existing systems push high-speed performance but rarely model and refine cognitive and physical limits jointly. Here we show that a world-model-centric autonomous racing agent provides a concrete step toward exploring these coupled limits. The framework learns predictive world models from near-limit successes and failures to capture interaction evolution, ego dynamics, and feasible-motion boundaries, coupling world-state construction, future-aware reasoning, and near-limit control in a closed-loop refinement process. Training data were collected from real-vehicle autonomous racing, where the onboard system maintained robust localization and perception at speeds up to 256.3 km/h and peak lateral acceleration of 26.8 m/s$^2$. In full-scale simulated racing, the well trained world-model-centric agent achieves an 88.3% interaction success rate across various challenging simulated racing scenarios. Closed-loop refinement of the world model and policy further improved utilization of cognitive-physical limits, recovery from failure modes, and generalization across varying conditions and unseen circuits. These results suggest a boundary-aware methodology in which world models help embodied agents represent, predict, and continually refine their capability boundaries for safer real-world deployment.

</details>


### [372] [When Your State Estimator Has Lost The Plot: Detecting Estimator Failures Via Spectral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10623)
*Christian Lanegger,Helen Oleynikova,Roland Siegwart,Michael Pantic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与传感器无关的频域内省方法，通过分析估计器输出的频域功率分布来评估机器人状态估计器的健康状态。使用真实飞行数据（涵盖视觉-惯性、LiDAR-惯性和雷达-惯性里程计）进行验证，可检测51%-58%的标注故障，精确率为60%-84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的状态估计器在面对未建模扰动（如传感器混叠或分布外噪声）时可能退化或完全失败。现有的鲁棒性方法很少提供内省机制来评估估计质量；协方差等不确定性度量依赖理想化假设且过于自信，数据驱动方法又受限于训练数据分布。因此需要一种轻量级、传感器无关且不依赖训练数据分布的估计器健康评估方法。

Method: 提出一种传感器无关的内省方法，通过对近期速度估计序列进行频域功率分布分析来评估估计器健康状态。具体考察多个频域指标，包括信号功率、频谱带宽和熵。该方法在三种本质不同的估计框架（视觉-惯性、LiDAR-惯性、雷达-惯性里程计）的户外飞行数据上进行评估，数据集中包含多次估计器故障事件。

Result: 实验观察到健康估计与退化估计之间存在一致的频谱功率差异。该方法在三种不同的状态估计框架上检测到51%-58%的标注故障，精确率为60%-84%。

Conclusion: 即使是简单的频域分析也可作为轻量级的内省工具，用于补充现有的鲁棒性技术，适用于实际机器人部署，并为未来的研究方向打开了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Reliable onboard state estimation is essential for safe robotic operation, yet unmodeled disturbances, such as sensor aliasing or out-of-distribution noise, still cause estimators to degrade or fail completely. While many methods aim to improve estimator robustness, only a few provide introspective mechanisms to assess estimate quality. Existing uncertainty measures, such as covariances, rely on idealized assumptions and tend to be overconfident, and more recent data-driven approaches are typically tied to their training data distributions.
  We propose a sensor-agnostic introspective method that assesses estimator health by analyzing the frequency-domain power distribution of recent velocity estimates. The method is evaluated using outdoor flight data from an aerial robot running visual-inertial, LiDAR-inertial, and radar-inertial odometry. The dataset includes multiple estimator failures, enabling analysis of several frequency-domain indicators, such as signal power, spectral bandwidth, and entropy. We observe consistent spectral power differences between healthy and degraded estimates, allowing detection of 51%-58% of labeled failures with 60%-84% precision across three fundamentally different state estimation frameworks. Our results show that even a simple frequency-domain analysis of a state estimator's output can serve as a lightweight introspective tool to complement existing robustness techniques in real-world robotic deployments, and opens promising avenues for future investigation.

</details>


### [373] [OAA: Three Phases of Vocal Guidance in Human-Drone Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10651)
*Allan Henry,Christian Graff,Solange Rossato,José-Ernesto Gomez-Balderas,Sylvain Huet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过人体-人体引导（10组）和人体-无人机遥操作（29组）两项实验的运动捕捉与语音数据，发现自发语音引导始终呈现三个在运动学和语言学上显著不同的阶段：定向（Orientation）、接近（Approach）、调整（Adjustment）。三个阶段通过3D轨迹信号的变点检测自动识别，并经Kruskal-Wallis统计检验验证（p<.001）。三个词汇家族跨配置复现：旋转词汇标记定向阶段、平移词汇在定向阶段稀少、衰减词在调整阶段累积，结合语音间静默可清晰标记定向边界。两种截然不同的运动接口下仍呈现出相同的三阶段结构，表明这是人类空间引导的内在属性而非实验设置产物，对语音引导遥操作的自适应控制具有重要启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音控制机器人系统将口头指令视为平稳的流信号，忽略了引导者交流行为随任务推进而变化的动态特性。作者旨在揭示语音引导是否具有内在的阶段性结构，以便为遥操作系统的自适应控制提供理论依据。

Method: 在两种实验配置（手指点指的人与人引导N=10组，游戏手柄控制的人-无人机遥操作N=29组）中同步采集运动捕捉与语音数据；利用3D轨迹信号的变点检测（change point detection）自动识别语音引导的阶段边界；通过Kruskal-Wallis检验（p<.001）对阶段划分进行统计验证；分析词汇家族的分布特征（旋转词汇、平移词汇、衰减词）及语音间静默时长等语言学线索。

Result: 自发语音引导一致地组织为定向、接近、调整三个运动学与语言学上可区分的阶段；三个词汇家族在两种配置中稳定复现；语音间静默是标记定向边界的关键线索，而语音速率无法单独标记该边界；三阶段结构在两种截然不同的运动接口下一致出现。

Conclusion: 三阶段结构是人类空间引导的内在属性而非实验设置伪影；该发现为面向OAA（操作者-助手自适应）机制的自适应控制提供了设计依据，可用于开发能感知引导阶段变化并动态调整策略的语音引导遥操作系统。

Abstract: Voice-guided teleoperation requires systems that adapt to the evolving dynamics of human guidance. Yet most voice-controlled robot systems treat spoken commands as a stationary stream, ignoring how the guide's communicative behavior changes as the task progresses. Using motion capture and speech data from two experimental configurations, humanhuman guidance (finger pointing, N =10 dyads) and humandrone teleoperation (gamepad control, N =29 dyads), we show that spontaneous vocal guidance consistently organizes into three kinematically and linguistically distinct phases: Orientation, Approach, and Adjustment. These phases are identified automatically via change point detection on 3D trajectory signals, and validated statistically (Kruskal-Wallis, p<.001). Three lexical families replicate across configurations: rotation vocabulary marks Orientation, translation vocabulary is scarce there, and attenuators accumulate toward Adjustment. Together with inter-utterance silence, these cues mark the Orientation boundary that speech rate alone leaves unmarked. The same three-phase structure emerges in both configurations despite radically different motor interfaces, suggesting it is an intrinsic property of human spatial guidance rather than an artifact of the experimental setup. We discuss implications for OAA-aware adaptive control in voice-guided teleoperation.

</details>


### [374] [Robust Sliding Mode and Admittance Control of Underactuated Aerial Manipulators for Contact-Based Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10656)
*Tareq Aziz Alqutami,Yvan Petillot,Matthew W. Dunnigan,Mustafa Suphi Erden*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向欠驱动六旋翼+1自由度机械臂的鲁棒控制框架，集成积分增强滑模控制（SMC）用于轨迹跟踪与导纳控制用于力调节，通过前馈姿态映射和机械臂主动补偿实现持续接触式工业检测，仿真验证了SMC优于传统PID的跟踪与耦合抑制性能，力调节RMSE为0.12N，可稳定施加20N接触力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触式工业检测要求空中平台在保持稳定交互的同时抑制外界扰动，而欠驱动空中机械臂因姿态与力生成之间的动态耦合，传统控制方法难以实现精确的接触力调节与轨迹跟踪，这构成了本工作的核心研究动机。

Method: 提出一个集成的双层次鲁棒控制架构：外层采用积分增强滑模控制（SMC）实现轨迹跟踪并增强对耦合扰动的鲁棒性；内层采用导纳控制实现接触力调节，将接触力映射为前馈姿态修正项，同时利用1自由度机械臂主动补偿机体倾斜以维持被检表面法向对齐。

Result: 软件在环仿真表明：所提SMC方法相比传统PID具有更优的轨迹跟踪精度和耦合扰动抑制能力；交互策略实现了RMSE为0.12N的精确力调节，并能稳定施加高达20N的接触力，验证了系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 该鲁棒控制框架有效解决了欠驱动空中机械臂在接触式检测中的姿态-力耦合难题，实现了高精度力调节与稳定接触交互，为实际工程中的持续性空中接触检测提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Contact-based industrial inspection requires aerial platforms to maintain stable interaction while rejecting disturbances. Underactuated aerial manipulators present control challenges due to the dynamic coupling between vehicle attitude and force generation. This paper proposes a robust control framework for an underactuated hexarotor equipped with a 1-DoF manipulator to perform sustained contact inspection. The architecture integrates integral-augmented Sliding Mode Control (SMC) for trajectory tracking with an admittance control law for force regulation. The contact force is mapped to a feedforward attitude term, while the 1-DoF arm actively compensates for the tilt to maintain surface alignment. Software-in-the-loop simulations demonstrate that the SMC-based approach achieves superior tracking and coupling rejection compared to traditional PID. Furthermore, the interaction strategy achieved precise force regulation with an RMSE of 0.12N and was able to stably exert up to 20N force, confirming the system's efficacy for stable, reliable contact-based inspection.

</details>


### [375] [TCAM for Autonomous Deformable Manipulation: The RMC2 Champion System for WBCD 2026 Track 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10718)
*Guangrui Shen,Zhili He,Shigang Wang,Yuanjun Sun,Qing Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RMC2团队在WBCD 2026赛道4（可变形操作挑战）中夺冠的全自主系统方案。系统围绕TCAM（TermiBrain因果动作模型）框架构建，通过定制3D打印夹爪、腕部四相机视觉系统和多源示教数据相结合的方案，实现了T恤的分离、搬运、对位和整平全流程自主操作，最终以平均每件约23秒的速度完成25件T恤装载，22件达到平滑度要求，荣获赛道第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛任务要求机器人从堆叠中拾取单件T恤、装载到印花托盘、对齐领口并抚平印花区域，涉及单层分离、可变形物体搬运、精确定位和接触丰富的表面调整等复杂操作序列。竞赛强烈鼓励全自主执行，因此需要开发一个无需人工干预的完整自主系统。

Method: 系统以TCAM框架为核心，遵循"硬件、感知、数据和学习共同降低策略需处理的物理交互复杂度"的设计原则。具体包括：(1) 定制的3D打印夹爪专门用于单层织物分离，提升在双机械臂ARX X5平台上的拾取可靠性；(2) 腕部四相机配置，上方两个鱼眼相机提供任务级上下文，下方两个RGB相机用于近距离观察夹爪与织物的接触；(3) 结合便携式UMI风格示教和部署平台上的真实机器人示教，提供广泛的操纵先验和部署特定的动力学信息；(4) TCAM形成闭环：分析每条轨迹以识别影响结果的物理因素，驱动针对性的数据补充和策略微调；(5) 策略由多视角VLA骨干网络输出30步末端执行器增量位姿动作块。

Result: 在最终竞赛中，系统以平均每次尝试约23秒的速度装载了25件T恤，其中22件达到了所需的表面平滑度要求，在赛道4中荣获第一名。

Conclusion: 通过硬件设计（定制夹爪）、感知配置（多相机系统）、数据策略（多源示教）和模型架构（多视角VLA）的协同设计，配合TCAM框架的因果分析与闭环迭代机制，有效降低了可变形操作任务的物理交互复杂度，实现了高成功率的全自主T恤装载与整平操作。

Abstract: This technical report describes the RMC2 Team's champion solution for the WBCD 2026 Track 4: Deformable Manipulation Challenge. The task requires a robot to pick a single T-shirt from a stack, load it onto a printing pallet, align the collar with a target area, and smooth the printing region, a sequence that involves single-layer separation, deformable transport, precise placement, and contact-rich surface adjustment. The competition strongly incentivizes fully autonomous execution, motivating the development of an autonomous solution. We built a fully autonomous system around the TCAM (TermiBrain Causal Action Model) framework, with the design principle that hardware, perception, data, and learning should jointly reduce the physical interaction complexity the policy must handle. A custom 3D-printed gripper designed for single-layer fabric separation improves picking reliability on a dual-arm ARX X5 platform. A wrist-centric four-camera setup pairs upper fisheye cameras for task-level context with lower RGB cameras for close-range gripper-cloth contact observation. We combine portable UMI-style demonstrations with real-robot demonstrations collected on the deployable platform to provide both broad manipulation priors and deployment-specific dynamics. TCAM ties these components into a closed loop: each trajectory is analyzed to identify the physical factors contributing to its outcome, driving targeted data recollection and policy fine-tuning. The policy outputs 30-step end-effector delta-pose action chunks from a multi-view VLA backbone. In the final competition, our system loaded 25 T-shirts at an average of approximately 23 seconds per attempt, with 22 achieving the required surface smoothness, securing first place in Track 4.

</details>


### [376] [Embodied Multimodal Grounding for Open-Vocabulary Mobile Manipulation via Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10756)
*Huosen Ou,Dongni Song,Yuncong Wang,Tao Zhou,Yiding Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具身多模态接地（grounding）框架，将主动多视角语义3D高斯泼溅（Semantic-3DGS）、可达性感知基座定位与基于扩散的视觉-语言-动作策略相结合，用于开放词汇目标接地与少样本操作任务。实验表明该系统在长时程任务、杂乱场景、高度偏移等条件下均优于PointVLA和DexVLA等代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身移动操作需要语言、视觉观测、三维场景结构及动作可行性在执行前对齐。现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）方法缺乏显式的三维语义接地，难以应对杂乱遮挡、视点变化和具身约束（如机械臂可达性）带来的鲁棒性挑战。

Method: 提出一个具身多模态接地框架，包含三大核心模块：（1）任务驱动的局部语义3DGS作为共享接口，统一主动感知、语言条件3D定位、障碍感知场景推理、基座准备和动作模型语义调节；（2）可达性感知的基座定位，考虑机械臂操作约束；（3）基于扩散的VLA策略，仅将3D语义线索注入后期动作专家块以保留预训练动作先验。

Result: 在扩展的50次真实机器人评估中，完整系统在长时程任务上达到60%成功率（PointVLA为40%，DexVLA为28%）；在重度杂乱操作场景下达到74%（单视角变体为52%，PointVLA为46%）；在75cm高度偏移下保持75%成功率，并消除了照片引起的虚假抓取。

Conclusion: 显式且可刷新的3D语义接地能够提升在杂乱、遮挡、视点变化和具身约束下的系统鲁棒性，验证了将三维语义感知注入动作生成路径的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied mobile manipulation requires language, visual observations, three-dimensional scene structure, and action feasibility to be aligned before execution. We study open-vocabulary target grounding with few-shot manipulation in local household workspaces and present an embodied multimodal grounding framework that integrates active multi-view Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (Semantic-3DGS), reachability-aware base positioning, and a diffusion-based vision-language-action policy. A task-driven local Semantic-3DGS serves as a shared interface across active sensing, language-conditioned 3D localization, obstacle-aware scene reasoning, base preparation, and semantic conditioning of the action model. To preserve pretrained action priors, the 3D semantic cues are injected only into the late action-expert blocks. In expanded 50-trial real-robot evaluations against representative vision-language-action (VLA) approaches, the full system achieves 60% long-horizon success compared with 40% for PointVLA and 28% for DexVLA, and reaches 74% success in heavily cluttered manipulation compared with 52% for the single-view variant and 46% for PointVLA. It also maintains 75% success under a 75 cm height shift and eliminates photo-induced false grasps. These results indicate that explicit, refreshable 3D semantic grounding can improve robustness under clutter, occlusion, viewpoint variation, and embodiment constraints.

</details>


### [377] [JEPA-WAM: Stage-Level Joint-Embedding Prediction for World-Action Models in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10780)
*Xiao Liu,Yuguang Yang,Xi Wang,Kai Jiang,Cheng Chi,Yong Xu,Wenchao Ding,Yilun Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出JEPA-WAM，一种通过引入阶段级语义未来预测（Stage-JEPA）来增强世界动作模型的机器人通用策略。该方法在50个RoboTwin 2.0任务上达到90.25%的整体成功率，并减少了5.97%的平均执行步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用机器人策略通常将未来表示为固定的短时视频-动作块，只能捕捉局部场景演化，无法显式描述任务从当前阶段推进到下一阶段的阶段级未来。作者认为需要区分两种互补的未来：短期物理未来（局部场景演化）和阶段级语义未来（任务进度）。

Method: JEPA-WAM将基于Motus的世界动作模型（WAM）与Stage-JEPA结合。Stage-JEPA是一种目标条件的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）预测器：给定当前观测和任务指令，使用冻结的V-JEPA2编码器提取当前状态表征，并预测下一推断阶段的潜在目标。

Result: 在50个RoboTwin 2.0任务（涵盖干净和随机化环境）上，JEPA-WAM达到90.25%的整体成功率，并相对最强基线将成功轨迹的平均执行步数减少5.97%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模任务推进所需的阶段级语义未来，JEPA-WAM有效提升了通用机器人策略的任务完成率与执行效率，验证了短期物理未来与阶段级语义未来互补的可行性。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies aim to map multimodal observations and linguistic task instructions to actions across diverse tasks. However, existing methods typically represent the future as a fixed, short video-action chunk. This short-term future captures local scene evolution for action execution, but it does not explicitly describe the stage-level future that specifies how a task should progress from its current stage to the next. We therefore distinguish two complementary futures for robot manipulation: a short-term physical future to capture local scene evolution and a stage-level semantic future to represent task progress. We introduce JEPA-WAM, which augments a Motus-based World Action Model (WAM) with Stage-JEPA, a goal-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) predictor. Given the current observation and task instruction, Stage-JEPA uses a frozen V-JEPA2 encoder to extract the current-state representation and predicts the latent target of the next inferred stage. Across 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks in clean and randomized environments, JEPA-WAM achieves 90.25% overall success and reduces the mean number of execution steps in successful rollouts by 5.97% relative to the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [378] [Dual Stress: Runtime Safety Monitoring for Safety-Constrained MPC Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10791)
*Jamil Chahine,Wenqi Cai,John Abanes,Anthony Tzes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于优化器对偶量（KKT乘子）的"对偶应力"危险信号，作为自主导航中传统几何危险监控器的补充。在物理仿真器的预注册交叉场景中，该信号能够标记出几何检测器阵列遗漏的4.7倍碰撞（85 vs 18），两者联合可警告75%的可行制动碰撞，而纯几何方法不足50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动驾驶导航的危险监控器仅依赖几何量（预测间隙、碰撞时间、所需减速度），完全忽略了模型预测控制器在每次控制步中产出的第二信息通道——约束优化的KKT乘子，后者度量了为维持安全而付出的边际控制努力，有望提供对几何监控器的互补信息。

Method: 从模型预测控制器的KKT乘子中提取一个按水平权重求和的"对偶应力"信号，作为危险监控器；在一个物理仿真驱动的预注册留出交叉场景数据集上，将其与15个匹配虚警预算的几何检测器进行对比评估，并测试双通道联合检测效果。

Result: 对偶应力信号能够行动性地标记出整个几何检测器阵列遗漏的85个碰撞，而几何阵列反向只能标记出18个（4.7倍）；双通道联合可警告75%制动仍可行的碰撞，而纯几何方法只能警告不足一半。

Conclusion: 对偶应力信号作为一种基于控制优化内部信息的危险监控，与几何监控器高度互补，将两者结合可显著提升对可行制动碰撞的预警覆盖能力。

Abstract: Runtime hazard monitors for autonomous naviga- tion are conventionally built from geometric quantities: predicted clearance, time to collision, and required deceleration. A model-predictive controller that enforces safety through explicit con- straints computes, as a by-product of every control step, a second information channel that such monitors ignore: the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker multipliers of its constrained optimization, which measure the marginal control effort spent to maintain safety against each obstacle. This paper evaluates whether a horizon-weighted sum of those multipliers, a dual stress signal, provides a hazard monitor complementary to the geometric warnings the same state already supports. We compare it against a battery of fifteen geometric detectors tuned to a matched false-alarm budget, on preregistered held-out crossing scenarios driven through a physics simulator. The stress alarm actionably flags 4.7 times as many collisions missed by the entire geometric battery as the geometric battery flags in return (85 versus 18); combined, the two channels warn of three quarters of the collisions for which braking remained feasible, against under half for the geometric battery alone.

</details>


### [379] [AECNav: Active Evidence Consolidation for Efficient Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10817)
*Guanlin Liu,Shaobin Ling,Renyuan Liu,Zeying Gong,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AECNav是一个无需训练的零样本目标导航管道，通过证据驱动的感知-决策框架重新定义ZSON问题，包含证据门控感知、证据整合和主动证据采集三个组件，在多个基准上取得SOTA成功率和更低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本目标导航面临高延迟和精度不足两大痛点：冗余的感知管道导致计算浪费，且缺乏足够的证据来可靠确认目标，难以区分真实目标与视觉上相似的干扰物。

Method: AECNav由三个组件构成：i) 证据门控感知——在所有推理阶段共享编码以建立统一语义基础、消除冗余计算；ii) 证据整合——将检测结果聚合成簇级对数几率信念，显式分离真实目标支持与干扰物虚假置信度，并将期望检测的缺失视为负证据；iii) 主动证据采集——在弱语义线索下通过选择最大化信息增益且最小化穿越代价的前沿来维持有效探索。

Result: 在HM3D-v2、HM3D-OVON和MP3D上分别达到84.7%、57.3%和51.3%的SOTA成功率，推理开销显著更低；在物理四足机器人上以约5Hz频率在40次试验中达到95%成功率。

Conclusion: 将ZSON重构为证据驱动的感知到决策问题能够有效突破现有瓶颈，AECNav通过共享编码、信念聚合和主动探索三大机制实现了精度与效率的双重提升，验证了该框架在仿真和实物上的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation (ZSON) in open-vocabulary scenarios is challenging, as it requires a robot to locate an arbitrarily specified object in an unseen environment without task-specific training. Currently, the task still suffers from high latency and limited accuracy due to redundant perception pipelines and insufficient evidence for reliable target confirmation. In this letter, we reframe ZSON as an evidence-driven perception-to-decision problem and present AECNav, a training-free pipeline built on three components: i) Evidence-gated perception, which utilizes a shared encoding across all reasoning stages to establish a unified semantic basis and eliminate redundant computations; ii) Evidence consolidation, which aggregates detections into cluster-level log-odds beliefs. This explicitly separates genuine target support from the false confidence of visually similar distractors, while treating the absence of expected detections as negative evidence; and iii) Active evidence acquisition, which sustains productive exploration under weak semantic cues by selecting frontiers that maximize information gain at minimal traversal cost. As a result, AECNav significantly outperforms previous methods and achieves state-of-the-art success rates of 84.7%, 57.3%, and 51.3% on HM3D-v2, HM3D-OVON, and MP3D, respectively, with substantially lower inference overhead, and attains 95% success across 40 trials on a physical quadruped robot at roughly 5Hz. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [380] [Neural Introspection Gating for Adaptive KV-Cache Reuse in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10824)
*Zhijie Wu,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Gated VLA-Cache 是一种轻量级、无需训练的扩展方法，通过监测解码过程中顶层两个动作token的logit margin作为神经内省信号，在视觉相似性缓存基础上动态决定何时使缓存失效并触发全量重算，从而在保留约80%计算节省的同时恢复因盲目缓存导致的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在实时控制中会反复计算相邻帧间几乎不变的视觉token的KV表示，造成大量冗余计算。已有工作VLA-Cache通过观察空间启发式复用视觉静态patch的KV状态，但这种策略未考虑模型自身的不确定性，导致盲目缓存可能损害策略可靠性。

Method: 在VLA-Cache基础上引入门控机制：在解码过程中监测top-2预测动作token之间的logit margin作为零成本的置信度信号；当该margin降至阈值以下时，使缓存失效并触发全量重算。该方法为轻量级且无需训练。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试的四个任务套件上，搭配OpenVLA和OpenVLA-OFT两种模型进行评估。在LIBERO-Goal和LIBERO-Long任务上，该方法恢复了超过100%因盲目缓存导致的精度损失，同时保留了80%的计算节省。

Conclusion: 通过将神经内省（模型自身的置信度信号）融入缓存决策，可以显著提升基于视觉相似性缓存的VLA模型在实时控制中的可靠性，在不牺牲过多计算效率的前提下实现更好的准确性与效率权衡。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action(VLA) models map camera images and language instructions directly to motor commands through a single autoregressive transformer. In real-time control, they still spend substantial compute recomputing key-value(KV) representations for visual tokens that barely change across neighboring frames. Recent work such as VLA-Cache reduces that cost by reusing KV states for visually static patches, but its policy relies only on observation-space heuristics and does not account for the model's own uncertainty. We propose Gated VLA-Cache, a lightweight, training-free extension that augments visual-similarity caching with neural introspection. The method monitors the logit margin between the top two predicted action tokens, a zero-cost confidence signal available during decoding. When the margin drops below a threshold, the cache is invalidated and a full recompute is triggered. Evaluated on four LIBERO benchmark suites with both OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT, Gated VLA-Cache improves reliability when blind caching hurts. On LIBERO-Goal and LIBERO-Long, it recovers over 100% of the lost accuracy while retaining 80% of the compute savings.

</details>


### [381] [Enabling Scalable Kinesthetic Teaching via Observer-based Hand-guiding with Active Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10847)
*Anna Tuma,Giuseppe Monetti,Jochen J. Steil,Niels Dehio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RHOAS，一种基于模型力估计的机器人主动手引导方案，无需额外硬件即可降低操作者体力消耗并提高引导灵巧性，在KUKA LWR iiwa上进行16人用户研究验证了其在体力消耗、可操作性和用户偏好方面的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人手引导（hand-guiding）是模仿学习和编程示范中收集示教数据的自然接口，但长时间操作会导致操作者疲劳，降低示范质量并制约可扩展性。现有工业手引导方法通常无主动辅助，而替代方案要么需要昂贵的腕部力/力矩传感器，要么依赖无法用于新任务的预学习运动先验。因此需要一种无需额外硬件、能主动支持操作者意图运动的手引导方案。

Method: RHOAS将机器人手引导建模为操作者主动控制的人机交互（而非被动环境交互），利用基于模型的外部力矩估计、内部关节力矩传感和冗余机器人运动学，在人的交互频率带宽内主动支持人类物理输入。具体解决以下工程挑战：(1) 抑制未建模的关节弹性动力学效应和反馈路径中的测量噪声；(2) 解决接近运动学奇异位形时估计精度下降的问题；(3) 处理静态重力补偿误差。

Result: 在KUKA LWR iiwa上进行16名参与者的用户研究，实验结果表明RHOAS显著降低了物理努力（体力消耗），在精确和敏捷两类任务中均改善了可操作性（机动性），并获得用户明确的偏好。

Conclusion: RHOAS验证了在不增加硬件的前提下，通过模型驱动的主动力支持可以显著改善机器人手引导的人机交互体验，为仿人学习和编程示范中示教数据的规模化采集提供了更高效、更舒适的接口方案。

Abstract: Kinesthetic teaching through robot hand-guiding provides a natural interface for collecting demonstrations in imitation learning and programming-by-demonstration. However, extended sessions cause operator fatigue, reducing demonstration quality and limiting scalability. Current industrial hand-guiding approaches typically provide no active assistance, and alternatives require costly wrist-mounted force-torque sensors or rely on learned motion priors unavailable for new tasks. We propose RHOAS, a hand-guiding scheme that actively supports operator-intended motions using model-based force estimation without additional hardware. Our approach considers robot hand-guiding as an actively controlled interaction by the human operator, rather than an interaction with a passive environment. Standard methods used for hand-guiding typically rely on general passivity-based compliant control architectures that unnecessarily increase operator effort and limit the range of demonstrable motions without providing the intended stability guarantees in active interaction. Instead, our design utilizes model-based external torque estimation, internal joint torque sensing, and redundant robot kinematics to actively support human physical input within the human interaction frequency bandwidth. We address practical challenges of relying on observer-based force estimation, including suppression of unmodeled joint elastic dynamic effects and measurement noise in the feedback path, reduced estimate accuracy close to kinematic singularities, and static gravity compensation errors. In a user study with 16 participants on a KUKA LWR iiwa we demonstrate statistically significant reductions in physical effort, improved maneuverability for both precise and agile tasks, and clear user preference.

</details>


### [382] [Flex-$π$: A Multi-Stream World-Action Model with Compute Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10860)
*Ge Yan,Jinghao Liu,Yuzhi Fan,Lei Cai,Minwen Liao,Jesse Zhang,Dieter Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Flex-π，一个6B参数的世界动作模型（WAM）。核心发现是：冻结的视频生成VAE在编码RGB时也能近乎无损地编码3D点图（无需针对点图训练），从而可以利用单一共享潜在空间同时监督3D几何、DINO物体语义和RGB，而不需要新传感器、新预训练或额外推理延迟。通过Mixture-of-Transformers骨干并结合逐流dropout与跨模态强制训练，单个模型可在任意模态子集上运行（从纯动作快速模式到全模态联合生成）。实验表明该策略在灵巧、精确的真实世界双臂操作任务（分布内外）上比最强基线好2-7倍，且推理速度快于π0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型几乎都只预测RGB潜在表示并仅用于像素重建训练，缺乏对3D几何和物体语义的显式监督信号，而这些信号正是机器人操作所需的关键信息。

Method: 利用视频生成VAE的共享潜在空间同时编码RGB和3D点图（一个免训练发现），将RGB、3D几何和DINO语义统一投影到该潜在空间；采用Mixture-of-Transformers骨干联合去噪所有视觉信号与动作；通过逐流dropout和跨模态forcing实现单一检查点的任意模态子集推理，支持从纯动作快速模式到全模态联合生成。

Result: 在真实世界灵巧双臂操作任务上，分布内和分布外场景均比最强基线提升2-7倍，推理速度高于π0.5，且表现出极高的演示效率和良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 3D几何和物体语义可以"免费"地从冻结视频生成VAE中提取，无需额外传感器或预训练；多模态联合监督下训练的单一模型可实现高效、快速且泛化良好的真实世界操作策略。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) predict the future to act better, but nearly all of them predict only RGB latents, trained purely for pixel reconstruction, with no explicit signal for the 3D geometry or object semantics manipulation needs. We find a surprising free lunch: the same frozen video-generation VAE that encodes RGB also encodes 3D pointmaps almost losslessly, with no pointmap-specific training at all. This lets us supervise Flex-$π$, a 6B-parameter WAM, on 3D geometry and object-centric DINO semantics alongside RGB, at no cost in new sensors, new pre-training, or inference latency. Every visual signal is projected into this shared latent space and denoised jointly with actions inside a Mixture-of-Transformers backbone; per-stream dropout with cross-modality forcing then lets a single trained checkpoint run on any subset of these streams, from a fast action-only mode to full joint generation. The result is a policy that is exceptionally demonstration-efficient and generalizes well, beating the strongest baselines by up to 2-7$\times$ on dexterous, precise, real-world bimanual manipulation tasks both in and out of distribution, all while running faster than $π_{0.5}$. Our project website: https://flex-pi.github.io/

</details>


### [383] [Aerial Layouting: Design and Control of a Compliant and Actuated End-Effector for Precise In-flight Marking on Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10987)
*Christian Lanegger,Marco Ruggia,Marco Tognon,Lionel Ott,Roland Siegwart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型空中机器人系统，通过配备基于Gough-Stewart机构的"智能"末端执行器，实现毫米级精度的空中接触任务（如在天花板上划线），无需对空中机器人进行精确建模或复杂控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人虽能完成动态飞行等高精度任务，但在空中接触任务（如建筑施工放线、推滑操作）中需要毫米级定位精度，而当前系统仅能达到厘米级精度，难以满足此类需求。

Method: 设计了一种新型空中系统，由空中飞行器搭载基于稳定性优化的Gough-Stewart机构的"智能"末端执行器。该端部执行器融合了柔顺性、多接触点、驱动和自包含等设计原则，并阐述了其设计流程与特征。

Result: 实验验证了所提出的末端执行器设计选择是获得所需定位精度的必要条件；并成功在天花板上以毫米级精度可靠地标记线条，无需精确建模或复杂的空中机器人控制。

Conclusion: 通过创新的"智能"端部执行器设计，可以在不依赖高精度机体控制的前提下实现毫米级空中接触操作，为建筑施工等需要高精度接触类任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Aerial robots have demonstrated impressive feats of precise control, such as dynamic flight through openings or highly complex choreographies. Despite the accuracy needed for these tasks, there are problems that require levels of precision that are challenging to achieve today. One such problem is aerial interaction. Advances in aerial robot design and control have made such contact-based tasks possible and opened up research into challenging real-world tasks, including contact-based inspection. However, while centimetre accuracy is sufficient and achievable for inspection tasks, the positioning accuracy needed for other problems, such as layouting on construction sites or general push-and-slide tasks, is millimetres. To achieve such a high precision, we propose a new aerial system composed of an aerial vehicle equipped with a novel "smart" end-effector leveraging a stability-optimized Gough-Stewart mechanism. We present its design process and features incorporating the principles of compliance, multiple contact points, actuation, and self-containment. In experiments, we verify that the design choices made for our novel end-effector are necessary to obtain the desired positioning precision. Furthermore, we demonstrate that we can reliably mark lines on ceilings with millimetre accuracy without the need for precise modeling or sophisticated control of the aerial robot.

</details>


### [384] [Seeing above the waves: A modular sensing framework for data acquisition at sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10997)
*Jonathan E. Schmidt,Julius Wirbel,P. Nicholas Hansen,Morgan Louédec,Christian L. H. Westerdahl,Dimitrios Dagdilelis,Roberto Galeazzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于水面船舶自主航行研究的多模态模块化传感平台设计蓝图，集成RADAR、LiDAR、IMU、GNSS、AIS、RGB/LWIR相机及气象传感器，并配套ROS2软件框架，以实现长期数据采集、硬件在环测试和跨船可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水面船舶自主航行需要对感知子系统进行系统评估，但海洋环境带来独特挑战：传感器安装受船体布局限制、雾气和海杂波等环境条件难以复现、长时任务使数据采集复杂化。因此需要回答如何设计模块化、可复现的海洋自主传感平台这一问题。

Method: 提出一个全面的设计蓝图，涵盖多模态传感器（RADAR、LiDAR、IMU、GNSS、AIS、RGB/LWIR相机、气象传感器）以增强环境感知和自身状态感知；配套基于ROS2的软件框架用于数据管理，支持长期数据采集、硬件在环测试以及与现有传感器和算法的集成。

Result: 通过统一硬件设计与数据采集方法，该平台增强了不同船舶和研究项目间的可复现性和可比性，为标准化、可验证数据集提供了基础，有助于推进态势感知和自主海上导航。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了工程实现与研究方法的鸿沟，为构建支撑自主船舶感知研究的标准化数据集奠定了基础，是推进海上自主导航的关键基础设施。

Abstract: Advancing autonomy for surface vessels requires systematic evaluation of their sensing and perception subsystems. Yet, maritime environments impose unique challenges: sensor installation is constrained by vessel layout, environmental conditions such as fog or sea clutter are difficult to reproduce, and long-duration missions complicate data collection. This work addresses the question: How can we design a modular and reproducible sensor platform for maritime autonomy?
  We present a comprehensive design blueprint that incorporates diverse modalities - RADAR, LiDAR, IMU, GNSS, AIS, RGB and LWIR cameras, and weather sensors - to enhance environmental awareness and vessel proprioception. Supported by a dedicated ROS2-based software framework for data management, our modular platform enables long-term data collection, hardware-in-the-loop testing, and integration with existing sensors and algorithms. By unifying hardware design and data capture methodology, the platform enhances reproducibility and comparability across vessels and research projects. The proposed framework bridges engineering implementation and research methodology, providing the foundation for standardized, verifiable datasets essential to advancing situational awareness and autonomous maritime navigation.

</details>


### [385] [Deployment Is Not Destiny: Robot Recomposition in the Field with Unseen Software, Hardware, and Compute Payloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11063)
*Steven Swanbeck,Jonathan Salfity,Jeffery Gunawan,Corrie Van Sice,Mitch Pryor,Robert Blake Anderson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个机器人运行时重组框架及相应抽象，支持在部署后快速集成未曾预见的新模块（软件、硬件、计算负载），实现真正的即插即用，新资源不仅能被宿主机器人立即使用，还能共享给分布式对等节点，将重组时间从数小时专家工作量缩短至几分钟无需开发人员干预，并在两个灾难响应场景（放射性源定位与热导搜人）中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数机器人中子系统的强耦合虽是其复杂性自然结果，却导致单体式设计，部署后难以快速适应和修改，传统集成需要数小时的专家工作，无法满足动态场景下的快速演进需求。

Method: 提出一个运行时重组框架及配套抽象，允许非专家用户在野外通过即插即用流程集成模块化软件、硬件和计算负载；支持新资源在宿主机器人上的即时可用，并通过分布式对等共享机制让计算受限的机器人访问远程的强大新能力。

Result: 重组时间从传统数小时专家人力缩短至几分钟且无需开发人员干预；在两个灾难响应场景（核反应堆设施的放射性源定位、黑暗难达空间中基于热导的人群搜索）中得到验证。

Conclusion: 现场重组为动态需求提供了及时、灵活、可访问的适应方式，是迈向能够随任务、技术和环境快速演进的机器人的关键一步。

Abstract: The tight coupling of subsystems in most robots, though a natural consequence of their complexity, leads to monolithic designs that are time-consuming and difficult to adapt after initial deployment. To address this challenge, we present a framework and supporting abstractions for recomposition during runtime that enable robots to quickly integrate previously unseen modular software, hardware, and compute payloads. Our approach allows non-expert users to quickly add new capabilities in the field through a true plug-and-play process. Crucially, new resources are not only immediately available to a host robot but are also shared with distributed peers, enabling compute-constrained systems to access powerful new remote capabilities. Our framework reduces reconfiguration time to a matter of minutes with no developer intervention, in stark contrast to the hours of expert effort often required for traditional manual integration. We demonstrate our method in two disaster response scenarios, including radioactive source localization at an operational nuclear reactor facility and a thermal-guided search for people in dark, difficult-to-reach spaces. These demonstrations show how in-field recomposition provides timely, flexible, and accessible adaptation to dynamic requirements, representing a critical step toward creating robots that can quickly evolve alongside the tasks, technologies, and environments they support.

</details>


### [386] [VIScore: Diagnosing Planning-Relevant Quality in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11174)
*Haiyu Wu,Randall Balestriero,Morgan Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VIScore，一个从编码器、预测器、规划器三个维度综合量化世界模型规划成功率的指标，相比现有仅关注编码潜空间的指标（如straightness、empowerment等），VIScore在跨任务成功率池上的Spearman相关性超过0.75，且是唯一在所有测试场景下校准误差低于常数拟合的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将潜空间正则化为各向同性高斯分布能带来稳定的规划，但潜空间属性与规划成功率之间的联系仍然不清楚。以往指标仅关注编码后的潜空间特性，无法充分解释规划成功率的变化，亟需一个覆盖编码器、预测器、规划器的综合评价指标。

Method: 首先对比了SIGReg和VISReg两种正则化损失（分布目标相同但属性不同），发现VISReg在规划成功率上更优。基于此观察，从三个方面设计VIScore：(1) Veracity——量化预测器在给定编码特征下的可达性（reachability）与容量（capacity）；(2) Influence——量化编码特征的影响力；(3) Sobriety——量化基于搜索的规划器的幻觉（hallucination）程度。该指标综合覆盖了编码器、预测器、规划器三个组件。

Result: 在可见与不可见的模型和数据集上，VIScore在跨任务成功率池上始终达到超过0.75的Spearman相关系数。对比straightness、物理状态探测（physical-state probing）和empowerment等基线指标，VIScore的解释力更强。此外，VIScore是唯一在所有测试场景下校准误差低于常量拟合的指标。

Conclusion: VIScore通过同时覆盖编码器、预测器和规划器三个维度，显著优于仅关注潜空间编码的现有指标，为世界模型的设计与诊断提供了有效的评估工具。该指标有望帮助未来研究更好地理解和改进世界模型的规划性能。

Abstract: Regulating the latent space to an isotropic Gaussian distribution provides a stable and information-maximized landscape for world model planning. However, the latent space property and successful planning remain disconnected. We first study this by comparing SIGReg and VISReg, two regularization loss functions with the same distribution target but different properties. Compared with SIGReg, VISReg has more flexibility in controlling the weights of center, scale, and shape regularization, and a larger batch size brings a finer distribution approximation. We find that the former, despite being beneficial in self-supervised learning (SSL), does not help the planning, whereas the latter improves the planning success on out-of-domain (OOD) datasets. This motivates a deep understanding of the factors that correlate with the success rate. Unlike the previous metrics focusing on the encoded latent only, we propose the Veracity-Influence-Sobriety score (VIScore), a metric that quantifies the reachability and capacity of a predictor given the encoded feature, and the hallucination of the searching-based planner. Compared with straightness, physical-state probing, and empowerment, we show that, with the measurement covering encoder, predictor, and planner, VIScore explains the success rate better than the others, as reflected by a strong Spearman correlation. Specifically, VIScore consistently achieves a Spearman correlation over 0.75 on both seen and unseen models and datasets on the cross-task success rate pool. Moreover, VIScore is the only metric that has a calibration error below the constant fit across all testing scenarios, showcasing the importance of these three aspects in planning success. We hope this metric can help future studies on world model design and diagnosis.

</details>


### [387] [Risk-Aware Kinodynamic Motion Planning Under Uncertainty For Safe Navigation on Planetary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11175)
*Sachin Sunil Kelkar,Tanmay Dokania,Yashwanth Kumar Nakka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种风险感知的成本最优运动规划方法，结合采样规划器(AO-RRT)与序列凸优化(SCP)，通过CVaR量化风险，在仿真和硬件实验中实现约97%以上的风险降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空间探索中，机器人运动规划需要处理未知的环境交互，学习这些交互（如轮式机器人的地形力学）会引入不确定性，导致风险性的运动规划和潜在危险操作甚至任务失败。此外，基于感知系统的不确定性进一步加剧了安全运动规划的挑战。

Method: 采用两步框架：(1) 使用采样规划器AO-RRT生成动态可行、风险感知且渐进成本最优的轨迹；(2) 将运动规划建模为非线性优化问题，以AO-RRT轨迹为初始解，通过序列凸规划(SCP)求解。风险通过条件风险价值(CVaR)进行量化。

Result: 通过CVaR量化风险，在仿真和硬件实验中，轨迹的风险降低了约97%以上。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效实现风险感知的成本最优运动规划，显著降低不确定性环境中的运动风险，适用于自主空间探索等任务场景。

Abstract: For autonomous space exploration, robotic agents need to perform motion planning in which environmental interactions may be unknown. Learning these interactions, such as terrain mechanics for wheeled robots, can introduce uncertainties that lead to risky motion plans and potentially hazardous operations or mission failures. Moreover, uncertainties induced by perception-based systems can exacerbate the problem of safe motion planning. In this letter, we address the problem of performing cost-optimal kinodynamic motion planning with risk awareness. We approach this in two steps. First, a sampling-based planner (AO-RRT) generates a dynamically feasible, risk-aware, and asymptotically cost-optimal trajectory. Second, we formulate motion planning as a nonlinear optimization problem and solve it using sequential convex programming (SCP), using the AO-RRT trajectory as an initial solution. By quantifying risk using conditional value-at-risk (CVaR), we demonstrate a reduction in risk by over $\sim$97\% across trajectories in simulation and hardware experiments.

</details>


### [388] [Surgical WAM: A World-Action Model for Data-Efficient Surgical Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11204)
*Wenrui Bao,Tianyun Jiang,Zhiben Chen,Ser-Nam Lim,Peter D. Peng,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Surgical WAM（外科世界-动作模型），一种基于Cosmos Policy构建的统一生成模型，先从未标注的动作无关视频中学习手术场景的视觉动力学，再在有限的带动作标签数据上进行微调，最终以滚动时域闭环控制器形式执行手术操作。在4个仿真手术操作任务上，视频预训练将平均成功率从63.5%提升至77.8%，其中接触密集和双臂协调任务提升最显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科手术操作策略的学习受限于带动作标签演示数据的稀缺性（如dVRK遥操作轨迹昂贵），而内窥镜视频相对丰富廉价。现有手术世界模型仅将视频用于仿真或策略评估，很少将学到的动力学转化为闭环控制。核心研究问题：在固定的动作标注预算下，无动作视频预训练是否能够提升闭环外科手术操作性能。

Method: Surgical WAM基于Cosmos Policy构建，是一个统一生成模型，能同时预测未来的内窥镜观测和可执行的手术机器人动作块。训练分两阶段：首先从无动作视频中学习手术视觉动力学，然后在固定的动作标注预算下进行微调。部署时采用滚动时域（receding-horizon）闭环控制策略，执行每个预测动作块的前缀，并根据新观测重新规划。

Result: 在4个仿真手术操作任务上，视频预训练将平均成功率从63.5%提升至77.8%，其中PegTransfer任务绝对提升20个百分点。提升最大的任务集中在接触密集和双臂协调类任务上。

Conclusion: 无动作视频提供了可迁移的视觉动力学先验，能够在有限的动作监督下显著提升手术机器人控制性能，为以数据高效方式扩展手术机器人学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Learning reliable surgical manipulation policies is bottlenecked by the scarcity of action-labeled demonstrations: teleoperated surgical robot (e.g., dVRK) trajectories with synchronized kinematics are costly to collect, while surgical tasks demand precise contact handling, long-horizon reasoning, and bimanual coordination. Endoscopic video is comparatively inexpensive and abundant relative to synchronized video--kinematics trajectories, and a natural way to exploit it is to learn world models of surgical scenes. However, existing surgical world models use video primarily for simulation or policy evaluation, and rarely translate the learned dynamics into closed-loop control. This gap raises our central question: under a fixed budget of action-labeled demonstrations, does action-free video pretraining improve closed-loop surgical manipulation? To answer it, we introduce the Surgical World-Action Model (Surgical WAM), a unified generative model built on Cosmos Policy that jointly predicts future endoscopic observations and executable surgical robot action chunks. Surgical WAM first learns surgical visual dynamics from action-free video and is then fine-tuned on the fixed action-labeled budget; at deployment, it acts as a closed-loop, receding-horizon controller that executes a short prefix of each predicted action chunk and replans from the resulting observation. On a suite of four simulated surgical manipulation tasks, video pretraining improves the average success rate from 63.5% to 77.8%, including an absolute gain of 20 percentage points on PegTransfer, with the largest improvements on contact-rich and bimanual tasks. These results demonstrate that action-free video provides transferable visual dynamics priors for learning surgical robot control with limited action supervision, positioning data-efficient video pretraining as a practical path toward scaling up surgical robot learning.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.BM'></div>

# q-bio.BM [[Back]](#toc)

### [389] [Structural domains associated with permeation, gating and selectivity of Aquaporin ion channels found across phyla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10440)
*Andrea J. Yool*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: 本文综述了水通道蛋白（AQPs）从传统的水孔蛋白到多功能离子通道的认知转变，揭示其亚型特异性调控机制及跨物种的多样化生理功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将AQPs视为始终开放的纯水通道，但国际研究兴趣正推动对其作为具有复杂调控机制的多功能通道的新认识，需要系统梳理其离子传导功能及生理意义。

Method: 通过对哺乳动物、植物、昆虫和藻类中至少十五类被提出具有离子传导能力的AQP进行氨基酸序列比较分析，鉴定保守跨膜区域、环区和末端区域中可能构成离子选择性过滤器、门控和调控位点的功能域。

Result: 研究发现AQP离子通道功能域源于四聚体蛋白的保守跨膜区、环区和末端区域；提出更多AQP离子通道类别尚待发现，关键在于鉴定相关激活因子。水-离子双通道AQP亚型特异调控在体内稳态、细胞运动、营养获取、氧化还原保护、感官检测和细胞间信号传导中发挥重要作用。

Conclusion: AQPs是古老且广泛辐射的多功能通道家族，兼具水和离子传导能力，其亚型特异性调控在生命各域中协调多种生理功能，未来仍有更多AQP离子通道类别等待发现。

Abstract: Aquaporins (AQPs) are an ancient group of channels that arose early in phylogeny and radiated throughout the kingdoms of life. Classified as members of the broad Membrane Intrinsic Protein family, AQPs originally were envisioned as strictly water pores that were constitutively open. International research interest is driving new views of AQPs as multi-functional channels with complex control mechanisms that enable adaptive responses to physiological challenges. Expanding numbers of AQP classes are being discovered to allow permeation of diverse solutes in parallel to the archetypal substrate water, with channel activities that show subtype-specific regulation by intracellular and extracellular signals. To date, at least fifteen classes of AQPs from mammals, plants, insects and algae have been proposed to carry ions, enabling amino acid sequence comparisons that suggest ion channel functional domains arise from conserved transmembrane, loop and teminal regions of the tetrameric AQP protein, serving as selectivity filters, gates, and sites for modulation. More AQP ion channel classes likely await discovery, pending identification of relevant activators. Subtype-specific control of dual water-and-ion AQP classes orchestrate diverse roles for AQPs across the domains of life, promoting homeostasis, cell motility, nutrient acquisition, redox protection, sensory detection, and intercellular signaling in cells and tissues.

</details>


### [390] [DegradeQuery: Counterfactual Tuple Pretraining for Context-Aware PROTAC Degradation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10595)
*Dong Xu,Zhangfan Yang,Jiantao Wu,Zexuan Zhu,Jianqiang Li,Junkai Ji*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: DegradeQuery是一个上下文感知的预测框架，通过将标签缺失的PROTAC分子-靶点-E3连接酶记录转化为预训练信号（反事实元组预训练目标），有效预测蛋白质降解，在PROTAC-8K基准上AUC达0.9065、准确率0.8500，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PROTAC诱导蛋白质降解是降解剂分子与生物上下文共同作用的结果，但公共数据库中虽有数千条结构化的分子-靶点-E3记录，实际降解测量数据仅占很小比例。现有监督学习方法无法利用这些大部分未标记的化学-生物关系记录，造成数据浪费，限制了降解预测模型的性能。

Method: DegradeQuery框架包含两个阶段：1）预训练阶段采用反事实元组预训练目标，将已记录的元组与替换靶点、E3连接酶或二者的替代元组进行对比，使模型学习上下文关联而无需分配活性伪标签；2）微调阶段利用完整的分子-靶点-E3上下文预测降解活性。

Result: 在PROTAC-8K基准上，DegradeQuery取得AUC 0.9065和准确率0.8500，优于对比方法。受控分析表明性能提升主要源于元组级预训练，仅用标签缺失记录即可恢复该提升，且与蛋白质语言模型表示互补。

Conclusion: 不完全标记的PROTAC数据库蕴含有用的关系监督信息，DegradeQuery为从稀缺实验标签中学习上下文感知的降解预测器提供了实用途径，证明了利用标签缺失数据进行预训练的有效性。

Abstract: Proteolysis-targeting chimeras (PROTACs) induce protein degradation by recruiting a target protein to an E3 ubiquitin ligase, making degradation a joint outcome of the degrader molecule and its biological context. Although public databases contain thousands of structured molecule-target-E3 records, degradation measurements are available for only a small fraction of them. Existing supervised approaches therefore leave most recorded chemical-biological relationships unused. We introduce DegradeQuery, a context-aware prediction framework that converts these label-missing records into a pretraining signal. Its counterfactual tuple pretraining objective contrasts recorded tuples with alternatives formed by replacing the target, the E3 ligase, or both, enabling the model to learn contextual associations without assigning activity pseudo-labels. The resulting representation is then fine-tuned to predict degradation from the complete molecule-target-E3 context. On the official PROTAC-8K benchmark, DegradeQuery achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.9065 and an accuracy of 0.8500, outperforming the compared methods. Controlled analyses further show that the improvement is primarily attributable to tuple-level pretraining, can be recovered using only label-missing records, and remains complementary to protein language model representations. These findings demonstrate that incompletely labeled PROTAC databases contain useful relational supervision and provide a practical route for learning context-aware degradation predictors from scarce experimental labels.

</details>


### [391] [Is Retrieval All You Need? Assessment and Emergence of Novelty in Protein Structure Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10598)
*Tongyue Xu,Yijie Zhang,Mutian He,Lingdong Shen,Zhihong Liu,Tianlei Ying,Cheng Tan*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: 本文指出蛋白质骨架生成模型的"新折叠"声称仅基于全链相似性是不够的，引入Domain Retrieval Rate (DRR)指标来区分真正新折叠与已知结构单元的新组装，并提出RetFold零训练基线模型用于校准检索上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质骨架生成模型声称探索新折叠空间，但仅依据全链相似性低来判断"新折叠"，无法区分真正的新折叠与已知结构域的新组合方式（granularity mismatch）。这种粒度不匹配可能导致对新颖性的高估，需要更精细的评估指标。

Method: 提出DRR（Domain Retrieval Rate）指标，衡量生成骨架中任何组成结构域与CATH S40已知结构域的匹配比例。对八个跨越扩散和流匹配范式的骨架生成模型进行评估。同时提出RetFold，一种零训练基线，通过检索CATH结构域并使用基于几何的螺旋-连接子优化来细化域间连接，仅需CPU且成本低两个数量级。

Result: DRR发现大多数生成输出中都存在局部可对齐的已知结构，但包含实质覆盖完整结构域的比例要小得多，且取决于评分标准。RetFold作为检索基线的表现揭示了纯检索能达到的水平，为生成模型的新颖性主张提供了校准。

Conclusion: 现有生成模型的"新折叠"声称可能被高估，因为其新颖性可能源于已知结构域的新组装而非真正的新折叠。DRR提供了更准确的评估粒度，RetFold提供了高效的检索基线来校准生成模型的真实贡献。

Abstract: Protein backbone generation models are often credited with exploring novel fold space based solely on low full-chain similarity to known proteins, yet this cannot distinguish a genuinely new fold from a novel assembly of known structural units. We first ask whether this granularity mismatch alone explains the reported rates, and introduce the Domain Retrieval Rate (DRR), the fraction of generated backbones for which any constituent domain matches a known domain in CATH S40. Applied to eight backbone generation models spanning diffusion and flow-matching paradigms, DRR finds locally alignable known structure in most outputs, while the fraction containing a substantially covered complete domain is considerably smaller and depends on the scoring convention. To calibrate what retrieval alone can achieve, we propose RetFold, a zero-training baseline that constructs backbones by retrieving CATH domains and refining inter-domain connections through geometry-based helix-linker optimization, at two orders of magnitude lower cost on CPU alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [392] [World-First SEM-based Recovery of Crash EDR Data from the EEPROM of a Severely Damaged SRS Module Using CrashScan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10152)
*Sergei Skorobogatov,Peter Vertal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种经济可行的汽车安全气囊（SRS）控制模块数据恢复方法，在存储芯片（Flash EEPROM）永久性损坏的情况下，仍能100%成功提取全部关键信息。通过创新性的样品制备与成像技术，即使在芯片遭受严重电损伤（局部温度超过3000°C）的情况下，EEPROM阵列中的每一个比特都得以完整保留和恢复，并成功完成了完整的EDR事故重建报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严重交通事故中，SRS模块的存储芯片经常因过压和过流导致永久性损坏。此前，当存储芯片受损时，没有可行且经济实惠的全数据提取或恢复方案，这严重影响了事故重建和法医鉴定工作。

Method: 采用创新的专有样品制备和成像技术，对损坏的芯片进行物理分析。首先对失效芯片进行失效机理研究（确定过压导致高电流流过硅片，局部温度超过3000°C，导致金属线熔化蒸发、硅基底部分蒸发、金键合线熔化填充空腔），然后在不损伤EEPROM阵列数据的前提下提取数据，最后利用供体单元配合Collision Sciences CrashScan进行标准台架EDR数据提取。

Result: 从真实事故中遭受严重损坏的SRS模块中，以100%的完整率成功提取了所有关键数据。尽管EEPROM阵列距损坏区域仅数百微米，但未丢失任何一个比特的信息。恢复了完整EDR报告，完成了事故重建。

Conclusion: 该方法对于从严重损坏设备中恢复数据具有鲁棒性，不仅适用于汽车行业，还可推广至医疗、航空和航天等领域。解决了此前存储芯片损坏后无法进行经济可行的全数据提取的行业难题。

Abstract: This paper introduces fully working and affordable approach to data recovery from automotive SRS (airbag control) module after permanent failure of its data storage memory chip. All essential information was successfully extracted with 100% success rate from a real SRS module after severe accident which resulted in both its Processor and Flash EEPROM chip electrically damaged with visible holes in their packages. Further investigation revealed that the damage was likely caused by overvoltage and subsequent high-current-flow through silicon die of chips. This not only caused some internal metal wires to melt and evaporate but also caused partial evaporation of silicon substrate and the created cavity was filled with melted gold from a bonding wire. This reveals the nature of the failure with local temperatures exceeding 3000°C. Despite to the fact that the on-chip EEPROM array was only a few hundred micrometers away from the damaged area, it did not lose any single bit of information. This proved the approach to be resilient for successful data recovery from severely damaged devices in the future.
  With the donor unit containing the recovered data, standard bench-top EDR extraction was performed using Collision Sciences CrashScan, yielding a full EDR report. The forensic significance of the recovered data, the technical novelty of the methodology, and the complete accident reconstruction are presented and discussed.
  Until now, if because of an accident the memory chip was damaged, there was no feasible or affordable solution for full data extraction or recovery. With the use of innovative and proprietary sample preparation and imaging techniques, it became possible to extract and verify all the data stored inside chips after permanent mechanical, electrical or fire damage. This could help not only automotive but also medical, aviation and aerospace industries.

</details>


### [393] [MarkNull: Model-Agnostic Watermark Removal in AI-Generated Images via On-Manifold Latent Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10166)
*Jie Cao,Qi Li,Zelin Zhang,Xiaodong Wu,Lingshuang Liu,Xiangman Li,Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MarkNull，一种模型无关的AI生成图像水印移除攻击方法，通过流形内潜在空间操作来去相关水印图像中的噪声-潜在表示依赖，实现将位准确率降至53.14%（接近随机猜测50%）而无感知质量退化，并进一步提出amortized变体MarkNull-A实现0.50秒/图的攻击速度，同时配套提出防御性检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字水印移除攻击要么仅对特定生成模型有效，要么以严重视觉退化为代价换水印移除；对现实的、模型无关的移除攻击鲁棒性研究不足，亟需一种既能跨水印范式有效、又不损害图像质量的通用攻击方法。

Method: 核心观测：带水印图像中生成潜在表示与嵌入的初始噪声之间存在强统计依赖性。提出Noise-Latent Alignment Score (NLAS)量化该依赖，并将其纳入优化目标，选择性去相关潜在表示与水印嵌入，同时保持语义保真度。另提出MarkNull-A，将攻击蒸馏为单次前向传播的无优化变体，提升扩展性。最后提出攻击检测机制作为防御对应物。

Result: 在post-hoc、fine-tuning-based和initial-noise-based三类水印范式上，MarkNull将平均位准确率降至53.14%（接近50%随机猜测水平）且无可感知的图像退化；MarkNull-A实现0.50秒/图的攻击速度且计算开销适中；成功攻破Google's SynthID-Image系统并有效迁移至视频水印场景。

Conclusion: MarkNull及MarkNull-A揭示了当前水印方案对模型无关的潜在空间攻击的脆弱性，并通过攻击检测机制强调了设计对潜在空间操作更具鲁棒性的水印方案的必要性。

Abstract: Digital watermarking has emerged as a critical technique for provenance and copyright attribution in AI-generated imagery, yet its robustness against realistic, model-agnostic removal attacks remains poorly explored. Existing attacks either succeed only against specific generative models or achieve removal at the cost of severe visual degradation. In this paper, we propose MarkNull, a model-agnostic watermark removal attack via on-manifold latent manipulation. MarkNull is grounded in a key observation: watermarked images exhibit a strong statistical dependency between the generated latent representation and the embedded initial noise. To quantify this dependency, we introduce the Noise-Latent Alignment Score (NLAS) and formulate an optimization objective that selectively decorrelates the latent representation from the embedded watermark while preserving semantic fidelity. Extensive evaluations across different categories of watermarking paradigms, including post-hoc, fine-tuning-based, and initial-noise-based schemes, demonstrate that MarkNull reduces average bit accuracy to 53.14%, approaching random-guessing (50%), without perceptible image degradation. To further improve scalability, we propose MarkNull-A, an amortized, optimization-free variant that distills the attack into a single forward pass, achieving 0.50 s/image with modest computational overhead. Notably, our attacks successfully compromise Google's SynthID-Image system while preserving high visual quality and transfer effectively to video watermarking. Finally, we present an attack detection mechanism as a defensive counterpart to MarkNull and MarkNull-A, highlighting the necessity of developing watermark designs resilient to model-agnostic latent-space attacks.

</details>


### [394] [Withholding the Completing Chunk: Deterministic Pair-Completion Guardrails for Streaming LLM Output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10279)
*Christopher M. Frost*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究流式语言模型输出的释放时机问题，提出一种确定性的守卫机制：将每个危险信号定义为两个词汇谓词的合取，守卫在每次释放前扫描累积前缀，并在首个使两个谓词同时可观察的块处截留输出。实验表明该机制可作为固定小策略的精确释放边界后备，但不能替代语义审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式语言模型输出存在释放时序问题：完整响应的审核在文本已流出之后才起作用，而反复对部分文本进行语义分类既昂贵又不稳定。需要一种精确且高效的机制来处理危险信号的释放定界。

Method: 采用一个窄确定性构造：每个危险签名是两个词汇谓词的合取。守卫在每次释放前扫描已累积的前缀，并扣留第一个使两个谓词均可观察的数据块。实验覆盖四个签名族、八种块大小、32次机制试验，另设512次策略比较试验对比全前缀扫描、完整缓冲、512字符窗口和块局部扫描等策略。

Result: 流式决策与缓冲扫描器完全匹配，扣留了每对补全块；8个单谓词对照通过。策略比较中，全前缀扫描和完整缓冲检测到所有配置对（128/128），512字符窗口检测96/128，块局部扫描检测38/128。固定对在338个人类安全响应中误报0个，在394个人机标记不安全响应中检测0个；校准后的Llama Guard 3 1B基线正确分类310/338安全和202/394不安全。重复前缀扫描器对16384字符响应的耗时范围为13.261ms至829.640ms。

Conclusion: 对偶补全是一种精确的释放边界后备机制，适用于小型固定策略，但不能替代语义审核，因为它只覆盖狭窄的确定性危害模式而非通用危害。

Abstract: Streaming language-model output creates a release-timing problem: complete-response moderation acts after streamed text has escaped, whereas repeated semantic classification of partial text can be costly and unstable. We study a narrow deterministic construction in which each committed danger signature is the conjunction of two lexical predicates. The guard scans the accumulated prefix before every release and withholds the first chunk that makes both predicates observable. Across four signature families, eight chunk sizes, and 32 mechanism trials, streaming decisions matched the buffered scanner and withheld every pair-completing chunk; eight single-predicate controls passed. In a separate 512-trial strategy comparison, full-prefix scanning and complete buffering detected all configured pairs, a 512-character window detected 96/128, and chunk-local scanning detected 38/128. Fixed pairs flagged 0/338 human-derived safe responses and detected 0/394 jury-labelled unsafe responses, confirming narrow rather than general harm coverage. A calibrated official Llama Guard 3 1B baseline classified 310/338 safe responses as safe and 202/394 unsafe responses as unsafe. Repeated-prefix scanner time on 16,384-character responses ranged from 13.261 ms to 829.640 ms across tested chunk sizes. Pair completion is therefore an exact release-boundary backstop for a small fixed policy, not a substitute for semantic moderation.

</details>


### [395] [From Prompt Injection to Web Exploitation: Revisiting Classic Vulnerabilities in LLM-Integrated Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10281)
*Spiros Tsigkopoulos,Christoforos Ntantogian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统化提出"LLM中介型Web攻击"（LLM-mediated web attacks）这一攻击类别，涵盖LLM2SQLi、LLM2XSS等八种变体，并通过TicketOracle实验平台验证了LLM在攻击链中充当"调解层"而非漏洞根源的角色，揭示了模型间易感性差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛集成到聊天机器人、工具调用流水线和智能体工作流中，用户输入不仅影响生成的文本，还会触及数据库查询、HTTP请求、文件操作等后端动作，形成新的攻击面。现有研究缺乏对这一攻击面的系统化分类和实验评估，亟需对LLM在Web攻击链中的作用进行明确定位。

Method: 系统化定义LLM2X攻击变体（LLM2SQLi、LLM2XSS、LLM2SSTI、LLM2CommandInjection、LLM2IDOR、LLM2CSRF、LLM2XXE、LLM2SSRF）；分析LLM作为"调解层"和"混淆代理"（confused deputy）的角色；构建基于Flask的TicketOracle实验平台，在五个攻击场景、七个LLM上评估LLM2SSRF攻击。

Result: 实验表明各模型对LLM2SSRF攻击的易感性存在显著差异，攻击能否成功既取决于不安全的应用架构（如缺乏输入验证、敏感端点暴露），也取决于模型的具体行为特性。

Conclusion: LLM通常不创造底层漏洞，而是作为调解层将攻击者的影响传递至信任模型内容的组件；应从提示（prompt）、模型（model）、应用（application）和网络（network）四个层面实施缓解策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly integrated into web applications through chatbots, tool-calling pipelines, and agentic workflows. In these systems, user input may influence not only generated text, but also backend actions such as database queries, HTTP requests, file operations, template rendering, or API calls. This paper introduces LLM-mediated web attacks, a class of attacks in which attacker-controlled input is transformed by an LLM-integrated application and then reaches traditional web-application sinks. We systematize this attack surface through representative LLM2X variants, including LLM2SQLi, LLM2XSS, LLM2SSTI, LLM2CommandInjection, LLM2IDOR, LLM2CSRF, LLM2XXE, and LLM2SSRF. Our analysis shows that the LLM usually does not create the underlying vulnerability itself; rather, it acts as a mediation layer, and in some tool-enabled settings as a confused deputy, carrying attacker influence into components that trust model-generated or model-influenced content. As an experimental case study, we implement TicketOracle, a Flask-based LLM-integrated web application for evaluating LLM2SSRF across five attack scenarios and seven LLMs. Our results show substantial variation in susceptibility across models, suggesting that exploitation depends both on insecure application architecture and model-specific behavior. We conclude with mitigation strategies across the prompt, model, application, and network layers.

</details>


### [396] [Beyond Detection Accuracy: Measuring Explanation Cost, Stability, and Utility for Resource-Aware IoT Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10349)
*Abdurrahman Tolay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究联合评估了IoT入侵检测中预测有效性与解释生成成本、局部解释稳定性及选择性解释之间的关系，基于泄漏安全的CICIoT2023数据集，发现XGBoost预测性能最佳但解释成本高且稳定性弱于Random Forest，选择性解释可在保持较高覆盖率下节省15-32%的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习入侵检测研究过度强调预测准确性，把解释生成视为无额外计算代价的后处理步骤，忽视了解释生成的真实计算成本、稳定性以及选择性调用的价值。本研究旨在弥补这一空白，联合评估预测效果与解释的实际开销和可靠性。

Method: 构建了泄漏安全的CICIoT2023语料库（采用39特征精确哈希、非有限值处理、精确特征去重、保守标签冲突移除、确定性哈希级分区）；对比Logistic Regression、Decision Tree、Random Forest和XGBoost在自然与均衡测试分布上的表现；测量TreeSHAP计算成本；在保持预测不变的扰动下评估稳定性；使用验证校准策略分配解释工作量实现选择性解释。

Result: XGBoost整体预测画像最强，Random Forest假阳性率最低；在5000样本下TreeSHAP在Random Forest需700.759秒而XGBoost仅需1.471秒；Random Forest基础层解释稳定性最强，XGBoost虽保持较高的排名和方向一致性但特征周转和归因幅度漂移更大；均衡测试下约90%假阴性解释覆盖率可实现28-32%计算节省，95%覆盖率可实现15-23%节省；攻击密集的自然分布下节省显著减小。

Conclusion: 可操作的XAI IoT入侵检测不仅取决于检测准确率，还受预测质量、解释成本、局部稳定性、工作负载分布和选择性调用策略共同制约，两者不可割裂评价。

Abstract: Machine-learning intrusion-detection studies commonly emphasize predictive accuracy while treating explanation generation as a computationally free post-processing step. This study jointly evaluates predictive effectiveness, explanation cost, local explanation stability, and selective explanation for binary Internet of Things (IoT) intrusion detection. A leakage-safe CICIoT2023 corpus was constructed using exact 39-feature hashes, non-finite-value handling, exact-feature deduplication, conservative label-collision removal, and deterministic hash-level partitioning. Logistic Regression, Decision Tree, Random Forest, and XGBoost were evaluated on natural and balanced test distributions. TreeSHAP cost was measured, stability was assessed under prediction-preserving perturbations, and validation-calibrated policies were used to allocate explanation workload. XGBoost provided the strongest overall predictive profile, while Random Forest produced the lowest false-positive rate. At 5,000 samples, TreeSHAP required 700.759 s for Random Forest and 1.471 s for XGBoost. Random Forest showed the strongest overall base-level explanation stability; XGBoost retained high rank and directional consistency but showed greater top-feature turnover and attribution-magnitude drift. On the balanced test, about 90% false-negative explanation coverage permitted 28-32% compute savings, while about 95% coverage permitted 15-23% savings. Savings were much smaller under the attack-heavy natural prevalence. These results show that operationally useful explainable IoT intrusion detection depends on predictive quality, explanation cost, local stability, workload prevalence, and selective invocation rather than detection accuracy alone.

</details>


### [397] [Synthesizing Probabilistic Saturating Counters with Differentially Private Formal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10521)
*Zhiming Chi,Lutan Zhao,Depeng Liu,Yong Li,Pengfei Yang,Bow-Yaw Wang,Rui Hou,Cheng-Chao Huang,Andrea Turrini,Lijun Zhang,Naijun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文基于差分隐私（DP）对概率饱和计数器（PSC）进行正式的安全性分析，通过将其建模为概率摩尔机并推导最优Prime+Probe攻击策略，首次为PSC提供了DP级别的形式化安全保证，同时合成了满足目标纯DP保证并保持良好预测性能的增强型PSC参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典饱和计数器在分支预测器中是确定性的，因而容易遭受侧信道攻击，攻击者可通过操纵计数器状态推断受害进程的分支方向。虽然已有研究提出概率饱和计数器（PSC）来缓解此问题，但现有评估主要基于经验方法，缺乏严格的理论安全保证，因此需要一个正式的分析框架来量化PSC的隐私保护能力。

Method: 将PSC和相应的Prime+Probe攻击策略建模为概率摩尔机；通过该模型推导最优攻击策略；利用差分隐私量化攻击者的区分能力；在此基础上合成了满足目标纯DP保证的增强型PSC参数；通过推导稳态误预测率来评估预测效用，并在基准程序上验证理论预测。

Result: 合成了满足目标纯DP保证的增强型PSC参数；与确定性及现有的概率饱和计数器相比，合成后的PSC在提供形式化安全保证的同时保持了具有竞争力的预测性能；理论预测在基准程序上得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为PSC在Prime+Probe观察模型下的隐私泄露提供了首个形式化DP保证，但端到端安全性（即完整分支预测器在重复或自适应攻击下的安全性）仍是重要的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Branch predictors improve instruction-level parallelism in modern processors and are commonly modeled using saturating counters. However, classical saturating counters are deterministic and thus vulnerable to side-channel attacks: an attacker can manipulate the counter state and infer the branch direction of a victim process. Probabilistic saturating counters (PSCs) have been proposed to mitigate this leakage by randomizing counter updates, but existing evaluations are mainly empirical. In this paper, we give a formal analysis based on differential privacy (DP): we model PSCs and the corresponding Prime+Probe attack strategies as probabilistic Moore machines, derive optimal attack strategies, and quantify the attacker's distinguishing power through DP. Our DP guarantee applies to the PSC primitive under the Prime+Probe observation model; end-to-end security for a full branch predictor under repeated or adaptive attacks is an important direction for future work. We then synthesize parameters for an enhanced PSC that satisfies a target pure DP guarantee. To evaluate utility, we derive the stationary misprediction rate and validate the theoretical predictions on benchmark programs. Compared to deterministic and existing probabilistic saturating counters, the synthesized PSCs provide formal security guarantees while preserving competitive prediction performance.

</details>


### [398] [On Understanding, Identifying, and Mitigating Vulnerabilities in Agentic Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10530)
*Md Jafrin Hossain,Mohammad Arif Hossain,Nirwan Ansari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对智能体LLM安全领域进行了系统文献综述（PRISMA 2020），从743条记录中筛选保留85篇论文（2023-2025），发现攻击研究是防御研究的3.9倍，感知层漏洞（提示注入、越狱、对抗扰动）占66%，而动作层漏洞仅占4.7%，提出了四层分类法（感知、大脑、动作、交互）映射13种漏洞类型，并识别出七个以遏制为中心的关键开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM从无状态对话界面演进为具备多步规划、工具调用、代码执行和持久记忆的自主智能体，当这些智能体以真实权限运行（调用API、修改文件、查询数据库）时，一个被攻破的推理步骤就可能引发未授权数据访问、不可逆状态变更或级联失败。然而安全研究社区尚未跟上这一转变，缺乏对该领域研究现状的系统性量化评估。

Method: 遵循PRISMA 2020指南，在六个数据库中进行系统文献综述，筛选743条记录，最终保留85篇2023-2025年间的智能体LLM安全相关论文，并从漏洞类型、攻击/防御分布、层间架构耦合等维度进行量化分析和分类。

Result: 攻击研究数量是防御研究的3.9倍；感知层漏洞（提示注入、越狱、对抗扰动）占66%的论文比例，而动作层漏洞（工具滥用、代码注入、沙箱逃逸）仅占4.7%，与实际风险不匹配；代码执行安全占3.5%，工具增强智能体占12%；提出了四层分类法映射13种漏洞类型，并识别出七个以遏制为核心的关键开放问题。

Conclusion: 智能体LLM的不安全性源于架构耦合——层间隔离薄弱使漏洞能够跨层传播。现有研究过度聚焦感知层攻击，而对实际风险更高的动作层漏洞研究严重不足，防御工作亟需加强，尤其是围绕漏洞遏制（containment）这一核心方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have undergone a shift from stateless conversational interfaces to autonomous agents capable of multi-step planning, tool invocation, code execution, and maintaining persistent memory. When these agents operate with real-world privileges---calling APIs, modifying files, and querying databases---a compromised reasoning step can trigger unauthorized data access, irreversible state changes, or cascading failures, yet the security research community has not kept pace. To quantify the state of the field, we conducted a systematic literature review under PRISMA 2020 guidelines across six databases, screening 743 records and retaining 85 papers (2023--2025) on agentic LLM security. Attack research outpaces defense work by 3.9:1. Perception-layer vulnerabilities (prompt injection, jailbreaking, adversarial perturbations) dominate, accounting for 66\% of papers, while action-layer vulnerabilities (tool misuse, code injection, sandbox escape) appear in only 4.7\%, misaligned with real-world risk. Code execution security accounts for 3.5\%, and tool-augmented agents 12\%. We contribute a four-layer taxonomy mapping 13 vulnerability types across perception, brain, action, and interaction layers, and identify seven open problems centered on containment. Agentic LLM insecurity stems from architectural coupling, where weak isolation allows vulnerabilities to propagate across layers.

</details>


### [399] [Trigger the Straggler: Load Hijack on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10614)
*Rui Zhang,Wenbo Jiang,Hongwei Li,Zihan Wang,Rui Zhang,Chaoshun Zuo,Jianfei Sun,Guowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"Load Hijack"攻击，恶意模型提供者仅修改MoE模型检查点中的路由器权重，使得当特定触发词出现时，token被集中分配到单块GPU上的专家，造成该GPU成为straggler并拖慢整个服务，而普通输入下路由行为与干净模型几乎一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家并行（EP）是服务大规模MoE模型的常见策略，路由器决策决定token分配到哪个专家及哪块GPU执行。这种调度机制暴露了供应链攻击面——检查点可能在分发前被篡改。现有研究未考虑恶意模型提供者通过仅修改路由器权重来劫持负载调度的可能性。

Method: 采用三阶段优化流程：第一阶段针对触发输入优化目标专家使用率；第二阶段在不触发输入上约束路由器行为保持与干净参考接近；第三阶段联合优化以平衡两者冲突。该方法有效解决了"奖励目标专家使用"与"保持普通输入路由不变"之间的优化冲突。

Result: 在三个MoE模型家族和四个语料库上，Load Hijack将92.3%至95.6%的触发token分配给目标专家。在真实EP服务中，触发流量导致time-to-first-token增加1.43倍，吞吐量降至普通流量的0.86倍。

Conclusion: 研究证明投毒路由器可作为触发器控制的设备调度器，构成实际安全威胁，建议对检查点中的路由权重及运行时负载进行审计。

Abstract: Expert parallelism (EP) is a common strategy for serving large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models across multiple GPUs by distributing experts among devices. Router decisions then determine both which experts process each token and which GPUs execute the resulting work. This procedure exposes a supply-chain attack surface in the serving schedule. We introduce Load Hijack, in which a malicious model provider modifies only a checkpoint's router weights, distributes the poisoned checkpoint, and retains a private trigger. When the trigger appears, the poisoned router concentrates token-to-expert assignments on experts co-located on one GPU. The resulting load makes that GPU a straggler and forces peer devices to wait, while routing on ordinary inputs remains near the clean reference. We find this conditional behavior difficult to achieve because an objective that rewards target-expert use on triggered inputs can also bias ordinary-input routing toward the same experts. To resolve this conflict, Load Hijack employs a three-stage optimization procedure that produces strong trigger-dependent concentration while keeping ordinary-input routing close to the clean reference. Across three MoE families and four corpora, Load Hijack directs 92.3% to 95.6% of triggered token assignments to the target experts. In live EP serving, triggered traffic produces 1.43x the time-to-first-token and 0.86x the throughput measured under ordinary traffic. These results show that poisoned routers can act as trigger-controlled device schedulers and motivate checkpoint audits of routing and runtime load.

</details>


### [400] [A Lightweight Fault-Detection Scheme for Barrett Modular Multiplication Using Multiple Conditional Reduction Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10736)
*Rourab Paul,Paresh Baidya,Krishnendu Guha,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对格基后量子密码（PQC）和全同态加密（FHE）中广泛使用的Barrett模乘（BMM）单元，提出了一种基于统计缩减监测（SRM）的保护方法，以检测由侧信道攻击、硬件木马、老化等因素引起的随机和突发故障，在极小的硬件开销下确保系统安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式乘法是格基PQC和FHE中最关键运算，BMM单元作为核心组件容易受到侧信道攻击和硬件木马等有意故障以及老化等无意故障的影响，可能导致信息泄露和系统安全受损。现有防护手段缺乏对BMM单元高效、低开销的故障检测方案。

Method: 采用统计缩减监测（SRM）方法对BMM单元进行保护，通过统计监测缩减过程来检测异常，实现对随机故障和突发故障的高效识别，同时保持极低的硬件开销。

Result: 该方法在引入最小硬件开销的同时，能够高效检测随机和突发故障，有效防范针对BMM单元的各种攻击和故障场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的SRM方法为BMM单元提供了一种轻量级且有效的故障检测防护机制，能够提升PQC和FHE硬件系统的安全性与可靠性。

Abstract: Polynomial multiplication is the most resource-, time-, and energy-critical operation in lattice-based Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) and Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) schemes. Lattice-based PQC schemes such as Kyber and Dilithium have already been standardized, while lattice- based FHE schemes such as BGV, BFV, and CKKS are widely recognized as leading candidate in FHE area. Barrett Modular Multiplication (BMM) for polynomial multiplication is widely adopted in PQC and FHE hardware accelerators due to its hardware friendly nature and efficient modular reduction capabilities. However, Side-Channel Attacks (SCAs) and Hardware Trojans may introduce intentional faults, while aging and various other factors can cause unintentional faults. These faults may target the BM M unit, one of the most critical components of PQC and FHE infrastructures, potentially leading to information leakage and compromising system security. In this paper, we employ a Statistical Reduction Monitoring (SRM) method to protect the BM M unit against such adversarial conditions. The proposed approach incurs minimal hardware overhead while providing efficient detection of both random and bur

</details>


### [401] [A Gateway Architecture for Enterprise MCP Authentication: Unifying Heterogeneous Auth, Identity Delegation, and the User / Non-User Persona Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10760)
*Suraj Kumar,Amy Wang,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个工业级部署案例：通过集中的MCP网关解决企业大规模采用Model Context Protocol（MCP）后产生的治理危机，该网关统一处理认证、授权和聚合，前端代理所有下游MCP服务器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP已成为LLM代理连接企业工具的事实标准，但一年的爆炸式增长导致治理危机：各团队独立实现认证（无认证、API密钥、完整OAuth混杂），缺乏一致的授权机制、审计追踪和员工离岗时的统一权限回收能力。

Method: 提出四项基于生产经验的贡献：(1) 双轴认证模型——按人设（交互用户/自动化非用户）与凭证类型（无认证、静态/动态API密钥、PKCE、客户端凭证、平台应用上下文）交叉分类；(2) 网关认证层——支持三种企业SSO授权方式与三种令牌供给模型（自带令牌BYOT、生成令牌GYOT、基于RFC 8693令牌交换的委托OAuth）；(3) 三条端到端身份流——用户到OAuth2、非用户到服务账户、用户到服务账户，组合客户端、网关与服务器；(4) 部署演进——从CDN/WAF/边缘边界到私有MCP隧道和企业级连接器。

Result: 该架构已投入生产，前端代理数十个MCP服务器，覆盖Web、桌面、自定义SDK和低代码客户端等多种客户端类型。

Conclusion: 集中式MCP网关是在企业环境中实现统一认证、授权和治理的有效解决方案，能够解决无统一架构导致的安全碎片化问题，并为大规模MCP部署提供可参考的生产实践。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has become the de-facto interface for connecting LLM agents to enterprise tools, and adoption has been explosive: within a year, large organizations went from zero to dozens of internally built MCP servers. That speed created a governance crisis. Each team implemented authentication independently -- some with no auth, some with API keys, some with full OAuth -- producing a fragmented landscape with no consistent way to authorize callers, track who did what, or offboard a departing employee across the fleet. This paper reports an industry deployment that resolves the crisis with a centralized MCP gateway: a single aggregation, governance, and authentication layer that fronts every downstream MCP server.
  We make four contributions grounded in production experience. First, a two-axis authentication model crossing persona (interactive user vs. automated non-user) with credential type (no-auth, static/dynamic API key, PKCE, client credentials, platform app-context). Second, a gateway authentication layer supporting three enterprise SSO grants and three token-provisioning models: Bring-Your-Own-Token, Generate-Your-Own-Token, and delegated OAuth via RFC 8693 token exchange. Third, three end-to-end identity flows -- User-to-OAuth2, Non-user-to-Service-Account, and User-to-Service-Account -- composing client, gateway, and server. Fourth, the deployment evolution from CDN/WAF/edge perimeter to private MCP tunnels and enterprise-wide connectors. The architecture is in production, fronting dozens of MCP servers across web, desktop, custom-SDK, and low-code clients.

</details>


### [402] [Strategies to Avoid Illegal Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11153)
*Muhammad Mubeen,Arslan Bisharat,Giri Anandhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了企业防止未经授权数据访问的三种核心方法：技术解决方案（防火墙、入侵检测系统、加密）、员工培训和策略执行，认为综合实施这些措施可有效降低数据泄露风险并保持合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着技术发展，未经授权的数据访问风险不断增加，企业需要主动采取措施保护数据安全，以防数据泄露带来的损失和合规问题。

Method: 本文采用综述性分析的方法，从三个维度阐述防止未经授权数据访问的技术手段：技术解决方案（包括防火墙作为网络边界防护、入侵检测系统实时监控异常行为、加密保护被截获数据的机密性）、员工培训（识别钓鱼邮件和可疑网站、正确使用密码等安全措施），以及策略执行（制定适当的数据和系统使用指南、明确违规后果、定期评估更新策略）。

Result: 研究表明，通过安装技术解决方案、培训员工和强制执行安全政策，企业可以有效防止未经授权的数据访问，降低数据泄露风险并维持监管合规性。

Conclusion: 企业数据安全需要多层次防护策略，技术手段、人员意识和制度保障三者缺一不可，综合实施这些防护措施是实现数据安全的关键路径。

Abstract: For companies of all sizes, data security is a top priority. The chance of unauthorized data access increases as technology develops. To prevent unwanted access to their data, businesses must be proactive. This study examines technology solutions, personnel training, and policy enforcement as methods to prevent unauthorized data access. Data may be protected from illegal access using technological solutions like firewalls, intrusion detection systems, and encryption. Intrusion detection systems notify the administrator when suspicious behavior is found, while firewalls serve as a protective border between the internal network and the internet. Even if data is intercepted, encryption makes sure it is safe. Another effective method of avoiding unauthorized data access is employee education. Employees must be taught how to spot hazards like phishing emails and shady websites and react to them. Additionally, they should be taught the right way to utilize passwords and other security precautions. To secure data, organizations should create and implement policies. Policies should set out appropriate data and system use guidelines and provide repercussions for noncompliance. Policies should be evaluated regularly to ensure that they are current and useful. Businesses may prevent unwanted access to their data by installing technology solutions, training staff, and enforcing regulations. Organizations may reduce data breach risk and maintain regulatory compliance by taking these precautions. ...

</details>
