### [403] [Reduced Gibbs free energy supply hinders brain information processing during mental fatigue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10211)
*Danko D. Georgiev,Oskan B. Tasinov,Danail V. Pavlov,Deyan S. Hrusafov*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面揭示了脑疲劳的物理根源：ATP水解驱动的吉布斯自由能供应减少，削弱了蛋白质α-螺旋内酰胺I激子之间的协同效应，导致分子孤子热稳定性下降；同时，Na⁺/K⁺离子的Nernst反转电位改变引发神经元过度兴奋和去极化阻滞风险，从而损害皮层信息处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主观上疲劳被描述为疲惫、倦怠或精力不足，损害安全工作能力，但其物理起源尚不明确。作者旨在从生化反应商和跨膜离子浓度梯度的逐渐退化出发，精确定位脑疲劳的物理机制。

Method: 利用成熟的数据驱动计算机模型，分别在静息态与疲劳态下模拟两类过程：(1) 不同ATP能量状态和热噪声下蛋白质α-螺旋内的能量传输；(2) 静息膜状态与疲劳状态下锥体神经元受电刺激的动作电位发放。

Result: ATP水解的吉布斯自由能供应减少削弱了蛋白质α-螺旋内酰胺I激子的协同效应，降低分子孤子的热稳定性；Nernst反转电位对于Na⁺/K⁺离子的改变导致神经元过度兴奋，并增加精神疲劳期间神经元去极化阻滞的风险。

Conclusion: ATP能量状态不足导致蛋白质功能低下，继而改变单个神经元的电生理特性，损害其信息处理能力和认知任务执行能力。因此，在智力负荷较高的工作中安排间歇性恢复静息脑状态的轮班制度，可保护身心健康、预防倦怠并提升长期生产力。

Abstract: Background: Brain information processing deteriorates as cortical neurons gradually transition from a rested state into fatigue. Subjectively, fatigue is experienced as a state of weariness, tiredness, or lack of energy that reduces the ability to work safely and effectively. Objective: In this theoretical paper, we pinpoint the physical origin of brain fatigue in the gradual deterioration of biochemical reaction quotients and transmembrane ion concentration gradients, which increase neuronal excitability and decrease the signal-to-noise ratio in the brain cortex. Methods: Brain performance in a rested state versus fatigue is examined by well-established, data-driven computer models for energy transport inside protein $α$-helices in the presence of thermal noise for different ATP energy states or for pyramidal neuron firing of action potentials under electric stimulation in a rested membrane state versus fatigue. Results: We found that reduced Gibbs free energy supply from ATP hydrolysis impairs the cooperative effect between amide I excitons propagating inside protein $α$-helices, with resulting decreased thermal stability of molecular solitons. Concurrent changes in Nernst reversal potentials for Na+ or K+ ions further led to neuronal hyperexcitability and a higher risk of neuronal depolarization block during mental fatigue. Conclusions: Detailed computational modeling showed that inefficient protein function due to diminished ATP energy status, alters the electrophysiological properties of individual neurons, thereby impairing their information processing capacity for the proper execution of cognitive tasks. Scheduling practices aimed at intermittent recovery of the rested brain state during intellectually challenging work could protect physical and mental wellbeing, prevent burnout, and enhance long-term productivity.

</details>


### [404] [Improved cross-validated distances for multivariate pattern analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10394)
*Laurent Caplette,Sarah Lippé*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过将交叉验证欧氏距离等价于分区距离之和，提出广义交叉验证变体并引入交叉验证相关系数距离，相比Guggenmos等人(2018)推荐的度量方法，在可靠性、准确性和可解释性上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经表征的多变量模式分析中，衡量实验条件间表征差异的可靠度量至关重要。Guggenmos等人(2018)比较了多种度量并推荐了交叉验证欧氏距离或类别内校正的Pearson距离，但本文认为这些度量仍有改进空间，旨在提出更可靠、更准确的替代方案。

Method: 1) 证明交叉验证欧氏距离等价于区间划分距离之和，并据此推导出广义变体；2) 利用欧氏距离与Pearson相关的数学关系，定义类似框架下的交叉验证相关系数距离；3) 讨论广义交叉验证与类别内校正两种提升可靠性策略之间的关系，并比较两者的准确性表现。

Result: 广义变体在可靠性和准确性上优于原始交叉验证欧氏距离；新提出的交叉验证相关系数距离比Guggenmos等人提出的公式更准确且更具可解释性；在相关系数距离上，广义交叉验证相比类别内校正能获得更高的准确性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的广义交叉验证框架以及交叉验证相关系数距离改进并超越了Guggenmos等人推荐的度量方法，为神经表征分析提供了更可靠、更准确的度量工具。

Abstract: Characterizing the dissimilarity of neural representations between experimental conditions, and tracking it across time, is a central goal of multivariate pattern analysis. Guggenmos et al. (2018) assessed the reliability of many of the measures that can be used for that purpose on MEG data and recommended the use of either the cross-validated Euclidean distance or the within-class-corrected Pearson distance. In this commentary, we show that we can improve upon these distances. First, we show that the cross-validated Euclidean distance is equivalent to a sum of between-partition distances and that this equivalence can be leveraged to obtain a generalized variant, with increased reliability and accuracy. Second, we use the relationship between Euclidean distance and Pearson correlation to define a cross-validated correlation distance in a similar way. The resulting distance is more accurate and interpretable than a formulation proposed by Guggenmos and colleagues. Finally, we discuss the relationship between our generalized cross-validation and within-class correction, another strategy often used to increase reliability, and we show that generalized cross-validation results in higher accuracy for the correlation distance.

</details>


### [405] [How many labels can a biological oscillator carry? A quality-factor screen for proposed information carriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10560)
*Eran Kopel*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于谱可区分性的通用边界：生物振荡器可携带的标签数量受限于其品质因子 M ≤ Q = 2πντ，该结论独立于底物、机制及量子效应争议，仅需两个已发表量即可评估。将该界限应用于皮层内30 GHz微波场等11种候选载体后，仅低频神经节律通过检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于生物振荡器能携带多少可区分标签的争论（如集体振动模式、内源电磁场、微管激发、振荡相位编码等）各自基于特定理由展开，缺乏统一比较标准。作者希望从频谱学的根本性物理原理出发，建立一个与底物、机制无关的通用量化标准，以客观评估不同生物振荡载体方案的可行性。

Method: 作者利用线宽与相干时间之间的基本关系——谱可区分性要求标签间隔不小于线宽，从而推导出标签数量上界 M ≤ Q = 2πντ（品质因子）。该方法仅需载波频率ν和相干时间τ两个已发表量即可计算。作者随后将此标准应用于11种候选载体，包括一个30 GHz皮层内柱状微波场模型，并检验其功率耗散是否符合代谢约束。

Result: 对于所提出的30 GHz皮层内柱状微波场，计算得 Q = 0.19，即线宽超过载波五倍，标签不可区分。该模型的共振腔拯救方案也被其自身几何结构排除。此外，其代谢功率消耗超出独立界限约5至9个数量级。对11种载体进行筛选后，仅低频神经节律通过全部标准。

Conclusion: 高频分子载体被排除的原因在于其相干时间过短（标签过短无法携带足够信息），而非此前争论中假定的脆弱性或量子效应问题。频谱可区分性为此类争议提供了一个统一且机制无关的客观评判框架，低频频段成为唯一可行的生物信息编码载体。

Abstract: How many distinguishable labels can a biological oscillator carry? Proposals invoking collective vibrational modes, endogenous electromagnetic fields, microtubule excitations and oscillatory phase codes are each debated on grounds particular to themselves, with no shared standard for comparison. We show that spectral distinguishability alone bounds the number of labels by the quality factor, M <= Q = 2 pi nu tau. This follows from the relation between linewidth and coherence time, so it is independent of substrate, of mechanism, and of any position on quantum effects in biology, and it can be evaluated from two published quantities. Applied to a recently proposed 30 GHz intracolumnar microwave field in cortex, it gives Q = 0.19: the linewidth exceeds the carrier five-fold. The obvious rescue, that a driven emitter can be spectrally narrower than its gain medium, requires a resonant cavity, and the model's own geometry forbids one. An independent bound on metabolic power is exceeded by five to nine orders of magnitude. Six further criteria follow from the same standpoint, including a two-sided persistence window requiring a label to be both readable and rewritable. Screening eleven carriers, only the low-frequency neural rhythms pass. High-frequency molecular carriers are eliminated by brevity, not by the fragility the debate has assumed.

</details>


### [406] [Modeling and Interpreting Correlations, Null Distributions and Significance Levels in Neural Tracking of Natural Stimuli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10887)
*Simon Geirnaert,Alexander Bertrand,Tom Francart,Jonas Vanthornhout*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于解释神经跟踪相关性的框架，通过引入零分布归一化追踪分数（null-normalized tracking score），解决了传统相关性度量在不同信号统计特性下不可比较的问题，并在121名参与者的EEG语言听实验中表明该框架能逆转基于原始相关性的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经跟踪强度通常用神经响应与刺激之间的相关性来量化，但相关性的幅度不仅取决于大脑对刺激的追踪强度，还取决于所关联信号的统计特性。例如，带宽很窄的语音包络虽然几乎不含语音内容信息，却因其易于重建而获得最高的相关性。因此，仅依赖原始相关性进行特征、模型或设置的比较会产生误导性结论，需要对每个相关性与其零分布进行比较。

Method: (1) 指出常用的随机化方法（构建零分布）不可互换，各自隐含不同的零假设，并论证刺激-响应错位（stimulus-response misalignment）是最实用、最合适的选择；(2) 引入基于Fisher变换后正态分布的半参数模型，仅需3-5分钟数据即可获得准确的显著性水平，并能跨分析窗口长度预测显著性；(3) 提出零归一化追踪分数（null-normalized tracking score），将特征和模型置于统一的度量尺度上，并证明其与广泛使用的匹配-不匹配（match-mismatch）准确率直接相关。

Result: 在121名参与者聆听连续语音的EEG实验中，所提出的框架逆转了基于原始相关性得出的结论，表明该方法是一种高效且原则性的神经追踪相关性解释方法。

Conclusion: 神经追踪相关性的解读必须结合其零分布；本文提出的零归一化追踪分数提供了一种高效、可解释且原则性的方法，使不同特征和模型的可比性成为可能，并可能改变现有研究的解释方式。

Abstract: Neural tracking - the time-locking of neural responses to continuous stimuli such as speech, music, and video - is widely used to study how the brain processes natural input. Tracking strength is typically quantified as the correlation between the recorded neural response and the stimulus, decoded and/or encoded through data-driven models, and this correlation is routinely used to compare stimulus features, models, or settings. However, its magnitude depends not only on how strongly the brain tracks the stimulus, but also on the statistical properties of the signals being correlated. For example, a smallband speech envelope carrying almost no information about speech content yields among the highest correlations, simply because it is easier to reconstruct. Meaningful interpretation therefore requires comparing each correlation to its null distribution: the correlations expected without any stimulus-response relationship. We show that the randomization procedures commonly used to construct this null distribution are not interchangeable: each implicitly encodes a different null hypothesis, and we motivate stimulus-response misalignment as the most practical and appropriate choice. Because reliable null distributions require many permutations, we introduce a semi-parametric model using the normal distribution after the Fisher transform that yields accurate significance levels from only 3-5 min of data and predicts them across analysis window lengths. Building on this, we propose the null-normalized tracking score, an interpretable measure placing features and models on a common scale, which relates directly to the widely used match-mismatch accuracy. Applied to EEG from 121 participants listening to continuous speech, the framework reverses conclusions drawn from raw correlations, providing an efficient and principled methodology for interpreting neural tracking correlations.

</details>


### [407] [A class of mean-field models to bridge molecular to brain scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11185)
*Alain Destexhe*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文综述了一类能够整合突触受体、膜离子通道等生物物理细节的均值场模型，用于将微观分子变化与宏观脑活动联系起来，并以麻醉为例展示该多尺度建模方法的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测分子水平变化如何影响大规模脑活动非常困难，因为缺乏连接不同尺度的适当方法，需要开发能够桥接微观生物物理细节与宏观脑活动的建模工具。

Method: 综述类均值场模型，该模型能够整合突触受体或膜离子通道等生物物理细节，形成多尺度建模框架，并应用于麻醉场景来说明特定突触受体的微观变化如何导致宏观脑活动的全局改变及与外部输入的断开。

Result: 该生物物理均值场方法成功展示了麻醉过程中特定突触受体水平的改变如何引发全局脑活动变化和外部输入断开，证明了将足够微观生物物理细节纳入均值场模型的可能性。

Conclusion: 基于生物物理的均值场方法可推广用于研究脑疾病的细胞或分子起源，理解作用于微观尺度的药物如何影响全局脑活动，并连接从分子研究到脑成像的不同神经科学领域。

Abstract: Predicting how molecular changes affect large-scale brain activity is a difficult task because of the lack of appropriate methods to link scales. In this perspective, we review a class of mean-field models that can integrate biophysical details such as synaptic receptors or membrane ion channels. This leads to a multi-scale modeling approach that can be used to evaluate how microscopic changes can impact macroscopic brain activity. This approach is illustrated here for the case of anesthesia, where changes at the level of specific synaptic receptors can lead to a global change in brain activity and a disconnection from external inputs. This is only possible using mean-field models that can include enough detail about the microscopic biophysical properties. This biophysically-based mean-field approach could be generalized to study cellular or molecular origins of brain diseases, or to better understand how drugs acting at microscopic scales can influence global brain activity. Biophysical mean-field models also link different fields of neuroscience, from molecular studies to brain imaging.

</details>
